Our experiments demonstrate that IdleSpec significantly improves agent performance in various agentic scenarios by effectively utilizing idle time. In particular, on the GAIA and FRAMES, IdleSpec achieves 55.6% average accuracy with Gemini-2.5-Flash, surpassing the vanilla baseline without idle-time usage by 5.1%. Furthermore, for MLE-Bench, which involves substantial delay from code executions, IdleSpec achieves performance gains of up to 9.1% on the Any Medal rate, highlighting its generalizability to long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [109] [ST-SimDiff: Balancing Spatiotemporal Similarity and Difference for Efficient Video Understanding with MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22158)
*Bingjun Luo,Tony Wang,Chaoqi Chen,Xinpeng Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对多模态大语言模型处理长视频时计算开销大的问题，提出将相似性用于识别冗余、差异性用于捕获关键事件的新视角，设计了无需训练的ST-SimDiff框架，通过时空图建模和并行双选择策略（相似性压缩静态信息、差异性保留动态事件），实现了高效的长视频处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要通过剪枝或合并标记来减少冗余，但这些方法主要关注重要性和相似性维度，存在两个关键缺陷：1）忽略了视频内容中的变化和转折点这一关键维度；2）缺乏对时空关系的协同建模。

Method: 提出了一个无需训练的框架ST-SimDiff，包含三个核心步骤：1）构建时空图以统一建模视觉标记之间的复杂关联；2）基于相似性的选择，使用社区检测保留代表性标记以压缩静态信息；3）基于时间差异的选择，精准定位内容变化点以保留捕捉动态变化的关键标记。通过并行的双选择策略实现以最少标记同时保留静态和动态内容。

Result: 实验结果表明，ST-SimDiff在显著降低计算成本的同时，性能显著优于现有最先进的方法。

Conclusion: ST-SimDiff通过结合相似性（压缩静态信息）和差异性（捕获关键事件）两种选择策略，在显著降低计算成本的同时，在长视频处理任务上优于现有最先进的方法。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) face significant computational overhead when processing long videos due to the massive number of visual tokens required. To improve efficiency, existing methods primarily reduce redundancy by pruning or merging tokens based on importance or similarity. However, these approaches largely overlook a critical dimension of video content, i.e., changes and turning points, and they lack a collaborative model for spatio-temporal relationships. To address this, we propose a new perspective: similarity is for identifying redundancy, while difference is for capturing key events. Based on this, we designed a training-free framework named ST-SimDiff. We first construct a spatio-temporal graph from the visual tokens to uniformly model their complex associations. Subsequently, we employ a parallel dual-selection strategy: 1) similarity-based selection uses community detection to retain representative tokens, compressing static information; 2) temporal difference-based selection precisely locates content-changing points to preserve tokens that capture key dynamic shifts. This allows it to preserve both static and dynamic content with a minimal number of tokens. Extensive experiments show our method significantly outperforms state-of-the-art approaches while substantially reducing computational costs. Our code is available in https://github.com/bingjunluo/ST-SimDiff.

</details>


### [110] [Adapting the Interface, Not the Model: Runtime Harness Adaptation for Deterministic LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22166)
*Tianshi Xu,Huifeng Wen,Meng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Life-Harness通过运行时接口适配而非模型参数更新来改进LLM智能体，在18个模型上实现88.5%平均相对提升，证明运行时干预可跨模型迁移并捕获可复用的环境结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体适配方法主要更新模型参数，但在确定性、规则治理领域中，许多失败源于模型-环境界面的不匹配，而非模型本身的能力不足。

Method: Life-Harness是一个生命周期感知的运行时harness，通过训练轨迹演化，将 recurring交互失败转化为跨环境契约、程序技能、动作实现和轨迹调控四个维度的可复用干预措施，且在评估时保持固定。

Result: 在7个确定性环境中覆盖126个模型-环境设置，116个获得改进；18个模型骨架上平均相对改进达88.5%；从Qwen3-4B-Instruct演化出的harness可迁移至其他17个模型。

Conclusion: Life-Harness证明了运行时接口适配是与模型中心化训练互补的替代方案，具有良好的跨模型迁移能力。

Abstract: LLM agents are shaped not only by their language models, but also by the runtime harness that mediates observation, tool use, action execution, feedback interpretation, and trajectory control. While existing agent adaptation methods mainly update model parameters, many failures in deterministic, rule-governed domains stem from mismatches at the model--environment interface. We propose Life-Harness, a lifecycle-aware runtime harness that improves frozen LLM agents without changing model weights or evaluation environments. Life-Harness evolves from training trajectories by converting recurring interaction failures into reusable interventions across environment contracts, procedural skills, action realization, and trajectory regulation, and remains fixed during held-out evaluation. On seven deterministic environments from $τ$-bench, $τ^2$-bench, and AgentBench, Life-Harness improves 116 out of 126 model--environment settings across 18 model backbones, with an average relative improvement of 88.5%. Harnesses evolved only from Qwen3-4B-Instruct trajectories transfer to 17 other models, showing that Life-Harness captures reusable environment-side structure rather than model-specific behavior. These results position runtime interface adaptation as a complementary alternative to model-centric agent training. Code is available at GitHub.

</details>


### [111] [Measuring Cross-Modal Synergy: A Benchmark for VLM Explainability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22168)
*Joël Roman Ky,Salah Ghamizi,Maxime Cordy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Problem: Current VLM explainability metrics suffer from "evaluation collapse" due to cross-modal redundancy in VLMs. Solution: Proposed Synergistic Faithfulness metric (F_syn) based on Shapley Interaction Index. Finding: VLM-specific explainers over-rely on visual features; adapted attention-based methods better capture cross-modal synergy.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current post-hoc explainers for VLMs are evaluated via unimodal perturbation metrics, but these fail because multimodal datasets contain language priors and modality biases. VLMs exhibit cross-modal redundancy, allowing them to answer visual queries using text alone. This causes unimodal metrics to penalize faithful explainers, triggering evaluation collapse with contradictory visual/textual rankings (Kendall's τ=-0.06).

Method: Introduced Synergistic Faithfulness (F_syn), a scalable metric based on Shapley Interaction Index that isolates joint Harsanyi dividend between modalities. Evaluated 8 XAI methods across 3 VLM architectures and 3 benchmark datasets.

Result: F_syn achieves high accuracy as a surrogate (ρ=0.92) with 24× computational speedup. VLM-specific explainers heavily over-index on visual salience and underperform adapted attention-based methods in capturing true cross-modal synergy.

Conclusion: This work establishes a rigorous evaluation framework for safely auditing VLM reasoning in high-stakes deployments by decoupling visual plausibility from cross-modal faithfulness.

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) map complex visual inputs to semantic spaces, but interpreting the cross-modal reasoning of VLMs currently relies on post-hoc explainers evaluated via unimodal perturbation metrics. We expose a limitation in this paradigm: because multimodal datasets contain language priors and modality biases, VLMs frequently exhibit cross-modal redundancy, allowing them to answer visual queries using text alone. Consequently, unimodal metrics penalize faithful explainers, triggering an evaluation collapse where visual and textual rankings fundamentally contradict each other. %(Kendall's $τ= -0.06$). To resolve this, we introduce Synergistic Faithfulness ($\mathcal{F}_{syn}$), a scalable metric rooted in the Shapley Interaction Index that strictly isolates the joint Harsanyi dividend between modalities, serving as a highly accurate surrogate ($ρ= 0.92$) while achieving a $24\times$ computational speedup. Evaluating 8 distinct XAI methods across 3 VLM architectures and 3 benchmark datasets, reveals that explainers proposed for VLMs heavily over-index on visual salience and significantly underperform adapted attention-based methods in capturing true cross-modal synergy. By decoupling visual plausibility from cross-modal faithfulness, this work provides a rigorous evaluation framework required to safely audit VLM reasoning in high-stakes deployments.

</details>


### [112] [LLM-Metrics: Measuring Research Impact Through Large Language Model Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22176)
*Si Shen,Wenhua Zhao,Danhao Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究团队提出LLM-Metrics，利用大语言模型的参数记忆来评估研究影响力。基于核心假设：高影响力论文在学术社区中获得更多曝光，进而进入LLM训练数据并形成更强的参数记忆。实验在17个LLM（0.5B-72B参数）和549篇CS论文上进行，证明了该方法的有效性（总体rho=0.1495, p=0.0004），同时揭示了模型规模与预测能力呈非单调关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Citation counts, while dominant, suffer from well-documented limitations including temporal lag, disciplinary bias, and Matthew effects. There's a need for a real-time, cross-disciplinary, citation-independent assessment metric for research impact.

Method: Four types of multiple-choice probes (title, author, method, and venue recognition) were designed and evaluated across 17 LLMs (0.5B to 72B parameters) from six vendors, testing 549 computer science papers published in 2023-2024. Statistical analysis used Spearman correlation against citation counts.

Result: Of 17 models, 15 produced positive predictions (9 significant at p<0.05). Overall Spearman correlation: rho=0.1495, p=0.0004. Stronger signal for 2024 papers (rho=0.1880), author-recognition probes showed strongest discriminative power. Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct (3B parameters) outperformed larger models with rho=0.1829.

Conclusion: LLM-Metrics provides a real-time, cross-disciplinary, citation-independent paradigm for research assessment. The approach leverages parametric memory of LLMs as an alternative metric for evaluating research impact, addressing limitations of traditional citation-based metrics.

Abstract: Citation counts remain the dominant metric for assessing research impact, yet they suffer from well-documented limitations: temporal lag, disciplinary bias, and Matthew effects. Here we propose LLM-Metrics, a research-impact assessment metric derived from the parametric memory of large language models (LLMs). The central hypothesis is that high-impact papers receive greater exposure in the academic community, that this exposure enters LLM training data in textual form, and that models consequently form stronger parametric memory of these papers. We designed four types of multiple-choice probes, covering title recognition, author recognition, method recognition, and venue recognition, and evaluated 549 computer science papers published in 2023-2024 across 17 LLMs spanning 0.5B to 72B parameters from six vendors. Of the 17 models, 15 produced positive predictions, 9 of which were significant at p less than 0.05, with an overall Spearman correlation of rho = 0.1495 and p = 0.0004 against citation counts. Three additional findings support the proposed mechanism. First, the predictive signal was stronger for 2024 papers, rho = 0.1880, whose citation counts were near zero at model-training time, reducing the plausibility of a simple reverse-causality explanation. Second, author-recognition probes showed the strongest discriminative power, consistent with an exposure-driven memory mechanism. Third, model scale and predictive power were non-monotonic: a 3B-parameter model, Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct, with rho = 0.1829, outperformed most larger models, supporting a selective-memory hypothesis in which the limited capacity of smaller models can serve as an effective information filter. LLM-Metrics offers a real-time, cross-disciplinary, citation-independent paradigm for research assessment.

</details>


### [113] [CLORE: Content-Level Optimization for Reasoning Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22211)
*Yuyang Wu,Qiyao Xue,Guanxing Lu,Weichen Liu,Zihan Wang,Manling Li,Olexandr Isayev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CLORE proposes a content-level optimization framework that improves reasoning efficiency by editing correct on-policy rollouts through an external augmentation model to delete unnecessary reasoning content while preserving answers, combined with reference-free DPO training. Experiments show improved accuracy-efficiency trade-off across multiple benchmarks and compatibility with existing methods.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Reinforcement learning post-training improves reasoning ability of large language models but often produces unnecessarily long, repetitive, or semantically opaque reasoning traces. Existing efficient reasoning methods mainly regulate response length through explicit budgets or length-aware rewards, leaving intermediate reasoning content weakly supervised.

Method: CLORE uses an external augmentation model to delete repetitive segments, illegible or task-irrelevant content, and superfluous reasoning after the solution is established, while preserving the final answer. The augmented-original pairs are optimized with an auxiliary reference-free DPO objective alongside standard policy-gradient training. By restricting augmentation to correct trajectories and performing local deletion, CLORE keeps edited rollouts close to the policy distribution and mitigates off-policy mismatch.

Result: Experiments on DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B and Qwen2.5-Math-7B across five mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that CLORE improves the accuracy-efficiency trade-off and remains compatible with multiple training methods. Content-level analyses demonstrate that CLORE reduces repetitive reasoning, illegible content, and post-answer exploration.

Conclusion: CLORE remains compatible with GRPO, DAPO, Training Efficient, and ThinkPrune. Content-level supervision serves as a complementary direction to length-level control for improving reasoning efficiency.

Abstract: Reinforcement learning post-training has improved the reasoning ability of large language models, but often produces unnecessarily long, repetitive, or semantically opaque reasoning traces. Existing efficient reasoning methods mainly regulate response length through explicit budgets or length-aware rewards, leaving intermediate reasoning content weakly supervised. We propose CLORE, a content-level optimization framework that improves reasoning efficiency by editing correct on-policy rollouts. CLORE uses an external augmentation model to delete repetitive segments, illegible or task-irrelevant content, and superfluous reasoning after the solution is established, while preserving the final answer. The resulting augmented--original pairs are optimized with an auxiliary reference-free DPO objective alongside standard policy-gradient training. By restricting augmentation to correct trajectories and performing local deletion, CLORE keeps edited rollouts close to the policy distribution and mitigates off-policy mismatch. Experiments on DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B and Qwen2.5-Math-7B across five mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that CLORE improves the accuracy--efficiency trade-off and remains compatible with GRPO, DAPO, Training Efficient, and ThinkPrune. Content-level analyses further show that CLORE reduces repetitive reasoning, illegible content, and post-answer exploration, supporting content-level supervision as a complementary direction to length-level control.

</details>


### [114] [Evaluating Large Language Models as Live Strategic Agents: Provider Performance, Hybrid Decomposition, and Operational Gaps in Timed Risk Play](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22238)
*H. C. Ekne*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文通过多阶段定时Risk游戏环境评估LLM在真实agent场景中的表现，发现端到端系统行为（如目标追踪、执行转化）比单独规划能力更重要，Gemini的竞争优势源于其在执行层面的优势而非规划本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态基准测试无法捕捉LLM在实际系统中的真实行为；现有评估缺乏对重复循环、时间限制、格式约束和失败模式等真实部署场景的考量

Method: 在32场重复游戏 championship中比较各模型表现；通过标准化执行环境（Gemini Flash脚手架）分离规划与执行；分析保存的规划与执行轨迹研究机制

Result: Gemini-3.1-pro-preview在32场游戏中赢得20场（对抗GPT-5.1、Claude-opus-4-7和Kimi-K2.6），显著优于等强度假设；规划能力本身在各模型间接近相等；Gemini更频繁地参照终端目标且随胜利接近增强此行为；Gemini产生更多深度征服链

Conclusion: LLM性能差异主要来自端到端系统行为而非单独规划能力；Gemini在目标追踪和执行转化方面的优势是关键机制；建议将LLM评估框架从静态基准测试转向有界工作流组件评估

Abstract: Static benchmarks capture only part of how large language models behave in practice. Real systems place models inside repeated loops with time limits, formatting constraints, and failure modes. We study this setting in a timed multi-phase Risk environment with explicit victory targets and repeated planning and execution cycles. In a replicated 32-game cross-provider championship under frozen rules, gemini-3.1-pro-preview won 20 of 32 games against gpt-5.1, claude-opus-4-7, and kimi-k2.6, and the pooled winner distribution differs strongly from an equal-strength null (p approx 1.5 x 10^-5). We then separate planning from execution by standardizing execution on a cheaper Gemini Flash scaffold. Under this design, a pooled 32-game planner bakeoff is consistent with near-equality (p approx 0.821), which indicates that much of the earlier provider spread came from end-to-end system behavior rather than planning alone. To study mechanism, we analyze saved planning and execution traces from the provider championship. Gemini refers to the terminal objective far more often than the other models and increases that focus as victory approaches. Gemini also converts more turns into deep conquest chains, even though it is not the cleanest runtime. These results show that live-agent performance depends on objective tracking, execution conversion, cost, and runtime reliability, and they support evaluating LLMs as components in bounded workflows rather than as isolated benchmark respondents.

</details>


### [115] [Unlocking Proactivity in Task-Oriented Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22240)
*Hongbin Zhang,Ning Gao,Yuqin Dai,Ruiyuan Wu,Jinpeng Wang,Rena Wei Gao,Bingdong Tan,Shuzheng Gao,Zongjie Li,Chaozheng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对主动任务导向对话系统中智能体过于保守的问题，提出将用户潜在担忧作为核心训练信号，通过Cognitive User Simulator建模用户分层人格，并设计Simulator-Induced Asymmetric-View Policy Optimization方法将模拟器状态转换为训练目标，实现主动说服能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动任务导向对话（如外呼销售）需要说服型智能体主动探查用户担忧并引导对话接受；但后训练LLM天然保守， reward-shaping RL（如GRPO）仅重新加权已采样行为，无法根本改变被动策略。

Method: 构建Cognitive User Simulator（认知用户模拟器），将用户建模为包含可观察外部特征和隐藏内部担忧的分层人格；提出Simulator-Induced Asymmetric-View Policy Optimization，包含非对称On-Policy自我蒸馏和状态转移策略优化两个组件。

Result: 用户潜在担忧是一个关键训练信号，条件化于该信号可以解锁主动能力；该能力不受采样破坏；模拟器可生成忠实且多样的交互，并追踪说服进度。

Conclusion: 通过用户潜在担忧的条件化，可以解锁传统后训练方法无法实现的主动对话能力；Simulator-Induced Asymmetric-View Policy Optimization 提供了一种将模拟器状态转换为训练目标的可行范式。

Abstract: Proactive task-oriented dialogue (TOD), such as outbound sales, demands a persuasive agent that actively probes the user's concerns and steers the conversation toward acceptance within a bounded number of turns. Yet post-trained LLMs are inherently conservative, and reward-shaping RL (e.g., GRPO) struggles since it only re-weights what an already passive policy samples. We show that conditioning on the user's latent concerns unlocks proactive capability that no amount of sampling can undermine, establishing these concerns as a pivotal training-time signal. To operationalize this finding, we build the \textbf{Cognitive User Simulator}, which models each user as a stratified persona comprising observable external traits and hidden internal concerns. The simulator produces faithful and diverse interactions, while emitting per-turn state dynamics that track persuasion progress. We then introduce \textbf{Simulator-Induced Asymmetric-View Policy Optimization}, which converts the modeled concerns and the simulation state transition into complementary training objectives: (1) \emph{Asymmetric On-Policy Self-Distillation} that transfers concern-aware behavior from a privileged view of the same policy into its deployable, conversation-only view; and (2) \emph{State-Transition Policy Refinement} ...

</details>


### [116] [SciCore-Mol: Augmenting Large Language Models with Pluggable Molecular Cognition Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22287)
*Yuxuan Chen,Changwei Lv,Yunduo Xiao,Zhongjing Du,Daquan Zhou,Yukun Yan,Zheni Zeng,Zhiyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SciCore-Mol通过三个可插拔认知模块（拓扑感知、分子生成、反应推理）弥合LLM与分子数据之间的语义差距，使8B开源模型在多项化学任务上达到或超越私有大模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM处理异构科学数据（如分子）时面临根本性挑战：离散语言符号与拓扑分子或连续反应数据之间的内在差距导致文本推理中信息严重丢失和语义噪声。

Method: 三个深度集成的可插拔认知模块：(1)拓扑感知模块，(2)基于潜在扩散的分子生成模块，(3)反应感知推理模块。每个模块通过学习到的表示接口与LLM骨干耦合，实现比纯文本工具反馈更丰富的信息交换。

Result: 在多种化学任务上表现出色，涵盖分子理解、生成、反应预测和通用化学知识；8B参数开源系统在与私有大型模型的比较中具有竞争力，并在多个维度超越后者。

Conclusion: SciCore-Mol为LLM配备科学专业知识提供了模块化、可插拔、灵活编排的蓝图，对药物设计、化学合成和更广泛的科学发现具有直接意义。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are central to the one-for-all intelligent paradigm, but they face a fundamental challenge when dealing with heterogeneous scientific data such as molecules: the inherent gap between discrete linguistic symbols and topological molecular or continuous reaction data leads to significant information loss and semantic noise in text-based reasoning. We propose SciCore-Mol, a modular framework that bridges this gap through three deeply integrated pluggable cognitive modules: a topology-aware perception module, a latent diffusion-based molecular generation module, and a reaction-aware reasoning module. Each module is coupled to the LLM backbone through learned representation interfaces, enabling richer information exchange than is possible with text-only tool feedback. Our experiments on diverse chemical tasks demonstrate that SciCore-Mol achieves strong comprehensive performance across molecular understanding, generation, reaction prediction, and general chemistry knowledge, with an 8B-parameter open-source system that is competitive with and in several dimensions surpasses proprietary large models. This work provides a systematic blueprint for equipping LLMs with scientific expertise through decoupled, pluggable, and flexibly orchestrated modules, with direct implications for drug design, chemical synthesis, and broader scientific discovery.

</details>


### [117] [Meta-Soft: Leveraging Composable Meta-Tokens for Context-Preserving KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22337)
*Wei Luo,Yi Huang,Songchen Ma,Huanyu Qu,Jiang Cai,Mingkun Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Meta-Soft addresses limitations of fixed Soft Token-based KV Cache eviction by using a learnable meta-library with Gumbel-Softmax selection to dynamically synthesize targeted soft tokens, and redistributes dropped context information through attention-flow mechanism.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing KV Cache eviction methods using fixed Soft Tokens rely on static parameters and cannot adapt to different input prompts or capture complex task relevance. Additionally, permanently discarding evicted KV pairs causes irreversible information loss.

Method: Meta-Soft builds a meta-library with learnable orthogonal basis matrix, uses a Gumbel-Softmax selector network to generate differentiable sparse weights for synthesizing targeted k Soft Tokens from input features, appends these tokens to probe key information, and introduces an attention-flow based integration mechanism to redistribute semantic information from removed tokens into retained ones.

Result: Meta-Soft outperforms existing state-of-the-art eviction methods on multiple datasets.

Conclusion: Meta-Soft provides a new solution for KV Cache compression, outperforming existing state-of-the-art eviction methods.

Abstract: The KV cache used in large language models has linearly growing time complexity, so LLMs face memory blow-up and reduced decoding efficiency when they process long contexts.Current KV Cache eviction has become an important research direction; however, existing methods based on fixed Soft Tokens (e.g., Judge Q) rely on a static parameter set as the query to evaluate the importance of KV pairs, so they cannot adapt dynamically to different input prompts, and they cannot precisely capture complex and changing task relevance.Also, evicted KV pairs are discarded permanently, so this causes irreversible information loss and context breaks. To address this problem, we propose Meta-Soft, a dynamic compression framework based on probe-driven context integration. Specifically, we build a meta-library with a learnable orthogonal basis matrix $\mathcal{L}$, and we use a selector network with Gumbel-Softmax to produce differentiable sparse combination weights, so we dynamically synthesize the most targeted $k$ Soft Tokens from the input prompt features.We append these Soft Tokens to the end of the input sequence to probe key information. We also introduce an attention-flow based integration mechanism, which redistributes the semantic information of removed tokens into retained tokens, and this keeps the dropped context information effectively.Experiments on multiple datasets show that our method outperforms existing state-of-the-art eviction methods and provides a new solution for KV Cache compression.

</details>


### [118] [Scaling Observation-aware Planning in Uncertain Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22364)
*Adrian Zvizdenco,Arthur Conrado Veiga Bosquetti,Alberto Lluch Lafuente,Christoph Matheja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对可观测性问题的可判定片段（SSP和POP），提出了改进的参数合成方法以及基于POMDP分解的新型求解技术，显著提升了最优可观测性问题的求解效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不确定领域中，为智能体部署何种感知能力是一个基础工程挑战，需要在任务可实现性与硬件及处理高成本之间进行权衡。

Method: 提出了两种技术方法：1）改进了原有的参数合成方法；2）开发了基于POMDP分解的新求解方法，用于识别合理的观测函数。

Result: 通过实例规模和运行时间两个维度验证，新方法分别实现了3个和5个数量级的性能提升。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于POMDP分解的新方法，在实例规模和运行时间上分别实现了3个和5个数量级的性能提升，为可观测性工程提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Deciding which sensing capabilities to deploy on an agent in uncertain domains is a fundamental engineering challenge, in which one balances task achievability against the high costs of hardware and processing. This problem has previously been formalized as the Optimal Observability Problem (OOP), based on the well-known Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (POMDP) model for decision-making. This work studies (sub-)symbolic techniques to scale solving of decidable fragments of the OOP, namely the Sensor Selection Problem (SSP) and the Positional Observability Problem (POP). Besides improving the original approach based on parameter synthesis, we develop a new solving method that identifies sensible observation functions via decomposition of POMDPs, improving performance by 3 and 5 orders of magnitude for instance size and runtime, respectively.

</details>


### [119] [KAPPS: A knowledge-based CPPS Architecture for the Circular Factory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22457)
*Etienne Hoffmann,Jan-Felix Klein,Sören Weindel,Max Goebels,Sebastian Behrendt,Daniel Hernández,Ratan Bahadur Thapa,Jürgen Fleischer,Kai Furmans,Steffen Staab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对循环制造中异质材料和不确定性带来的挑战，提出KAPPS架构——一种基于本体力知识图谱的Cyber Physical Production System。该架构通过语义接口层实现跨异构系统的数据集成和推理，支持运行时的增量规划和约束执行，并通过异常检测和输送系统两个用例验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性制造依赖均质材料和预定义流程，而循环制造重新引入使用过的产品，这些产品具有异质性和不确定性。当前IT架构设计用于稳定结构和确定性执行，无法充分表示和管理单个组件在运行时的独特性，无法满足循环制造的需求。

Method: 采用设计科学研究方法，从五个互补视角推导出14个需求，据此设计KAPPS架构。KAPPS以本体论支撑的知识图谱作为统一数据骨干，结合语义接口层实现一致的数据和信息集成、推理和通信，并将知识图谱从集成层提升为工厂权威的写入时状态。架构包含约束执行和事件驱动规划模块。

Result: 通过两个已实现的用例验证了KAPPS的适用性：(1)通过知识图谱中介服务实现异常检测和学习；(2)在模块化输送系统中的运行时约束执行。

Conclusion: 该架构通过两个用例验证，并在14个需求上进行了评估，展示了在不确定性条件下的增量适应能力和人机知识交换能力。

Abstract: While linear manufacturing relies on homogeneous materials and predefined process sequences, circular manufacturing reintroduces used products with heterogeneous and uncertain conditions. This shift demands manufacturing systems capable of handling variable product states, dynamically reconfigurable processes, and the integration of human and machine knowledge. Conventional manufacturing IT architectures, designed for stable structures and deterministic execution, are unable to meet these requirements, as they cannot adequately represent and manage the uniqueness of individual components at runtime. Following a design science methodology for developing a Cyber Physical Production System for circular manufacturing, we derive 14 requirements from five complementary perspectives. Based on these requirements, we design KAPPS, a knowledge-based architecture that uses an ontology-grounded knowledge graph as a unifying data backbone, combined with a semantic interface layer to enable consistent data and information integration, reasoning, and communication across heterogeneous systems and services, turning the knowledge graph from an integration layer into the factories authoritative write-time state. KAPPS incorporates modules for constraint enforcement and event-driven planning, enabling incremental adaptation of execution plans under uncertainty and human-machine knowledge exchange. The applicability of KAPPS is demonstrated through two implemented use cases: (i) Anomaly detection and learning through knowledge graph mediated services and (ii) runtime constraint enforcement in a modular conveyor system. Subsequently, the architecture is evaluated against the 14 requirements (ed. abstract shortened)

</details>


### [120] [Compiling Agentic Workflows into LLM Weights: Near-Frontier Quality at Two Orders of Magnitude Less Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22502)
*Simon Dennis,Rivaan Patil,Kevin Shabahang,Hao Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Agent orchestration frameworks have proliferated, collectively exceeding 290,000 GitHub stars across LangGraph, CrewAI, Google ADK, OpenAI Agents SDK, Semantic Kernel, Strands, and LlamaIndex. All follow the same pattern: an external orchestrator above the LLM, injecting instructions and routing decisions every turn. Recent work has shown this architecture is dominated for procedural tasks by simply providing the procedure in a frontier model's system prompt [Dennis et al., 2026a], at the cost of consuming the context window, requiring a frontier model for every conversation, and exposing proprietary procedures to third-party providers. Compiling the procedure into the weights of a small fine-tuned model -- creating a subterranean agent -- should resolve all of these concerns, and prior work (SimpleTOD, FireAct, SynTOD, WorkflowLLM, Agent Lumos) has shown the technique works. Yet developer adoption has overwhelmingly favored orchestration. We identify three perceived barriers and address each empirically across travel booking (14 nodes), Zoom support (14 nodes, product-specific knowledge), and insurance claims (55 nodes, 6 decision hubs).

</details>


### [121] [LACO: Adaptive Latent Communication for Collaborative Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22504)
*Tianhao Chen,Yuheng Wu,Dongman Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Collaborative driving aims to improve safety and efficiency by enabling connected vehicles to coordinate under partial observability. Recent approaches have evolved from sharing visual features for perception to exchanging language-based reasoning through foundation models for behavioral coordination. Though communicating in language provides intuitive information, it introduces two challenges: high latency caused by autoregressive decoding and information loss caused by compressing rich internal representations into discrete tokens. To address these challenges, we analyze latent communication in collaborative driving under inherent limitations of multi-agent settings. Our analysis reveals agent identity confusion, where direct fusion of latent states entangles decision representations across vehicles. Motivated by this, we propose LACO, a training-free \textbf{LA}tent \textbf{CO}mmunication paradigm that seamlessly adapts pretrained driving models to collaborative settings. LACO introduces Iterative Latent Deliberation (ILD) for latent reasoning, Cross-Horizon Saliency Attribution (CHSA) for communication-efficient information selection, and Structured Semantic Knowledge Distillation (SSKD) to stabilize ego-centric decision making. Closed-loop experiments in CARLA show that LACO notably reduces communication and inference latency while maintaining strong collaborative driving performance.

</details>


### [122] [Towards Direct Evaluation of Harness Optimizers via Priority Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22505)
*Kai Tzu-iunn Ong,Minseok Kang,Dongwook Choi,Junhee Cho,Seungju Kim,Seungwon Lim,Geunha Jang,Minwoo Oh,Bogyung Jeong,Sunghwan Kim,Taeyoon Kwon,Jinyoung Yeo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种低成本设计（优先级排序）来直接评估harness优化器，通过让优化器对组件进行潜在性能影响排序来量化其能力。研究表明优化器的排序表现与其实际优化效果高度相关，证明优先级排序可作为可靠的能力预测指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前harness优化研究仅通过观察目标智能体的最终性能提升来评估优化器，这种间接的端点改进评估忽略了优化器在中间步骤的actions（这些actions通常是有误的且会阻碍智能体性能）。因此，无法判断harness优化是由优化器有知的更新actions驱动还是仅靠试错。这迫切需要对harness优化器进行直接评估。

Method: 采用优先级排序方法：要求优化器对给定harness中的组件（如工具）按其对智能体性能的潜在改进/阻碍能力进行排序，从而在步骤级别量化优化器能力，避免昂贵的rollouts或人工检查。

Result: 优化器的排序性能与其在实际多步harness优化中改进智能体的能力呈正相关，验证了优先级排序可作为优化能力的可靠预测指标。

Conclusion: Priority ranking可作为评估优化器能力的可靠预测指标；Shor数据集（182个人类验证场景）为评估工作提供了低成本基准。

Abstract: Harness optimization enables automated agent creation by having an optimizer agent iteratively update the harness of target agents. Despite its success, current studies evaluate optimizers solely by observing target agents' performance gains. This indirect end-improvement evaluation neglects optimizers' actions at intermediate steps, which are often erroneous and hinder agent performance. Therefore, it is unclear whether harness optimization is driven by optimizers' informed update actions or simply trial-and-error. This necessitates direct evaluation of harness optimizers. However, evaluating harness optimizers directly is non-trivial and costly due to the lack of oracle harnesses. To address this, we present a simple, low-cost design to directly evaluate them, namely priority ranking. By asking harness optimizers to rank components (e.g., tools) in a given harness by their potential to improve/hinder agent performance when updated, our design quantifies optimizer ability at the step level without expensive rollouts or manual examination. More importantly, optimizers' ranking performance correlates with their ability to improve agents in actual multi-step harness optimization, establishing priority ranking as a reliable predictor of optimization ability. Priority ranking is enabled by Shor, a collection of 182 human-verified optimization scenarios spanning across domains, designs, and time stages. Codes and data can be found at https://github.com/k59118/Harness_Optimizer_Evaluation.

</details>


### [123] [Meta-Learning for Rapid Adaptation in Reference Tracking of Uncertain Nonlinear Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22513)
*Jiaqi Yan,Ankush Chakrabarty,Niklas Schmid,John Lygeros,Alisa Rupenyan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: A meta-learning control framework using iMAML for reference tracking of uncertain nonlinear systems with limited data, combining offline meta-training from source systems and online adaptation on target systems via bi-level optimization, demonstrating superior performance over baselines.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Reference tracking for uncertain nonlinear systems faces challenges in collecting sufficient target system data. The goal is to design optimal controllers using limited target system data by leveraging offline data from source systems (sharing structural similarities) via meta-learning paradigm.

Method: A meta-learning-based control framework tailored from iMAML (implicit Model-Agnostic Meta-Learning), operating in two phases: (1) offline meta-training to learn aggregated representation capturing shared system dynamics from source data; (2) online meta-adaptation to fine-tune on target system with limited samples. Formulated as bi-level optimization with reduced storage complexity. Two learning algorithms are integrated: neural state-space model and deep Q-network.

Result: The proposed methods enhance control performance and consistently outperform baseline approaches, validated through both numerical simulations and hardware experiments.

Conclusion: The proposed framework is general and flexible, with numerical simulations and hardware experiments demonstrating enhanced control performance that consistently outperforms baseline approaches.

Abstract: In this paper, we address the problem of reference tracking for uncertain nonlinear systems. Since collecting data from the target system (i.e., the system of interest) is often challenging, our objective is to design optimal controllers using limited target system data. Meta-learning provides a promising paradigm by leveraging offline data from source systems (systems sharing structural similarities with the target system) to accelerate training and enhance control performance. Motivated by this idea, we propose a meta-learning-based control framework that tailors the implicit model-agnostic meta-learning (iMAML) algorithm to the control setting. The framework operates in two phases: an (offline) meta-training phase, where an aggregated representation is learned from source data to capture the shared system dynamics among similar systems, and an (online) meta-adaptation phase, where this representation is fine-tuned on the target system using only a few data samples and limited adaptation steps. We formulate this framework as a bi-level optimization problem and provide an efficient solution with reduced storage complexity and few approximations. The proposed framework is general, allowing various learning algorithms to be integrated. To demonstrate this flexibility, we propose two specific learning algorithms that can be incorporated into our framework based on a neural state-space model and a deep Q-network, respectively. The primary distinction between these approaches is whether explicit system identification is required. Numerical simulations and hardware experiments demonstrate that the proposed methods enhance control performance and consistently outperform baseline approaches.

</details>


### [124] [A Subjective Logic-based method for runtime confidence updates in safety arguments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22530)
*Benjamin Herd,Jessica Kelly,Clarissa Heinemann,João-Vitor Zacchi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: We present a method for dynamic quantitative assurance that enhances static safety cases with continuous, runtime-driven confidence updates. The method quantifies and propagates confidence across the development lifecycle by integrating design-time evidence and windowed runtime Safety Performance Indicators (SPIs) within a single Subjective Logic (SL)-based assurance case. At runtime, SPI evidence is continuously evaluated, and targeted claims are updated using a rule that increases confidence in the absence of violations and imposes prompt penalties when violations occur. This design prioritizes safety-relevant responsiveness over exact classical Bayesian posterior updates. We demonstrate the method using a simulation-based construction zone assist function, focusing on an ML-based construction cone detection component, and show how confidence evolves as SPI evidence is observed in operation.

</details>


### [125] [TerminalWorld: Benchmarking Agents on Real-World Terminal Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22535)
*Zhaoyang Chu,Jiarui Hu,Xingyu Jiang,Pengyu Zou,Han Li,Chao Peng,Peter O'Hearn,Earl T. Barr,Mark Harman,Federica Sarro,He Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TerminalWorld is an automated benchmark engine that extracts 1,530 real-world terminal tasks from 80,870 in-the-wild recordings. Testing 8 frontier models and 6 agents reveals a maximum pass rate of only 62.5%, with only weak correlation (r=0.20) to existing benchmarks, highlighting gaps in current terminal agent capabilities.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing terminal benchmarks rely on expert curation, which may not adequately capture authentic real-world developer practices. As developer workflows evolve, there is a need for benchmarks that can automatically and scalably evaluate agents in realistic terminal environments.

Method: TerminalWorld employs an automated data engine that reverse-engineers evaluation tasks from 80,870 in-the-wild terminal recordings. This process yielded 1,530 validated tasks across 18 categories and 1,280 unique commands, from which 200 representative tasks were manually curated into a Verified subset. Benchmarking was conducted across 8 frontier models and 6 agents.

Result: Current frontier models and agents perform poorly on authentic terminal workflows, with a maximum pass rate of only 62.5%. TerminalWorld shows weak correlation with existing expert-curated benchmarks (Pearson r=0.20), suggesting it captures distinct real-world capabilities.

Conclusion: TerminalWorld provides an authentic and scalable benchmark for evaluating AI agents in real-world terminal environments. Current frontier models achieve only up to 62.5% pass rate, indicating significant room for improvement. The weak correlation (r=0.20) with existing benchmarks highlights that TerminalWorld captures unique real-world terminal capabilities not covered by expert-curated benchmarks, making it a valuable complementary evaluation tool.

Abstract: We introduce TerminalWorld, a scalable data engine that automatically reverse-engineers high-fidelity evaluation tasks from "in-the-wild" terminal recordings. Processing 80,870 terminal recordings, the engine yields a full benchmark of 1,530 validated tasks, spanning 18 real-world categories, ranging from short everyday operations to workflows exceeding 50 steps, and covering 1,280 unique commands. From these, we curate a Verified subset of 200 representative, manually reviewed tasks. Comprehensive benchmarking on TerminalWorld-Verified across eight frontier models and six agents reveals that current systems still struggle with authentic terminal workflows, achieving a maximum pass rate of only 62.5%. Moreover, TerminalWorld captures real-world terminal capabilities distinct from existing expert-curated benchmarks (e.g., Terminal-Bench), with only a weak correlation to their scores (Pearson r=0.20). The automated engine makes TerminalWorld authentic and scalable by construction, enabling it to evaluate agents in real-world terminal environments as developer practices evolve. Data and code are available at https://github.com/EuniAI/TerminalWorld.

</details>


### [126] [Think Thrice Before You Speak: Dual knowledge-enhanced Theory-of-Mind Reasoning for Persuasive Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22602)
*Minghui Ma,Bin Guo,Runze Yang,Mengqi Chen,Yan Liu,Jingqi Liu,Yahan Pei,Xuehao Ma,Qiuyun Zhang,Zhiwen Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ToM-PD任务和ToM-BPD数据集，基于BDI框架显式建模多轮对话中心理状态的序列依赖关系，并提出TTBYS框架增强LLM的ToM推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM由于依赖简单提示策略和对ToM知识不足，难以捕捉心理状态间的内在依赖关系，导致表征碎片化和推理不稳定。

Method: 提出了TTBYS（Think Thrice Before You Speak）知识增强的逐步推理框架，利用显式和隐式先验经验来增强LLM对欲望、信念和说服策略的推断能力。

Result: 装备TTBYS的Qwen3-8B在欲望预测上优于GPT-5 1.20%，信念预测上优于22.80%，说服策略预测上优于16.97%。

Conclusion: TTBYS框架使Qwen3-8B在各项预测任务上显著超越GPT-5，案例研究证明该方法增强了推理的可解释性和一致性。

Abstract: Persuasive dialogue requires reasoning about others' latent mental states, a capability known as Theory of Mind (ToM). However, due to reliance on simple prompting strategies and insufficient ToM knowledge, existing LLMs often fail to capture the intrinsic dependencies among mental states, leading to fragmented representations and unstable reasoning. To address these challenges, we introduce the ToM-based Persuasive Dialogue (ToM-PD) task, grounded in the Belief-Desire-Intention (BDI) framework, which explicitly models the sequential dependencies among mental states in multi-turn dialogues. To facilitate research on this task, we construct a large-scale annotated dataset, ToM-based Broad Persuasive Dialogues (ToM-BPD), capturing fine-grained mental states and corresponding persuasive strategies. We further propose Think Thrice Before You Speak (TTBYS), a knowledge-enhanced stepwise reasoning framework that leverages both explicit and implicit prior experiences to improve LLMs' inference of desires, beliefs, and persuasive strategies. Experimental results demonstrate that Qwen3-8B equipped with TTBYS outperforms GPT-5 by 1.20%, 22.80%, and 16.97% in predicting desires, beliefs, and persuasive strategies, respectively. Case studies further show that our approach enhances interpretability and consistency in reasoning.

</details>


### [127] [Spreadsheet-RL: Advancing Large Language Model Agents on Realistic Spreadsheet Tasks via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22642)
*Banghao Chi,Yining Xie,Mingyuan Wu,Jingcheng Yang,Jize Jiang,Zhaoheng Li,Shengyi Qian,Minjia Zhang,Klara Nahrstedt,Rui Hou,Xiangjun Fan,Hanchao Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Spreadsheet-RL是一个强化学习微调框架，用于在真实Excel环境中训练专门的电子表格AI智能体。通过自动化数据收集管道、Spreadsheet Gym RL环境和Domain-Spreadsheet基准数据集，该框架将Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507在电子表格任务上的Pass@1从12.0%提升至23.4%，证明了RL微调在复杂电子表格自动化任务中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电子表格智能体主要依赖通用LLM的专门提示工程，这种设计在简单操作上有效，但难以应对真实世界中复杂、多步骤的工作流程自动化需求。

Method: 采用强化学习(RL)微调框架，在真实Microsoft Excel环境中训练专门的电子表格智能体。核心组件包括：(1)从在线论坛自动化收集配对起点-目标电子表格的可扩展管道；(2)Spreadsheet Gym多轮RL环境，通过Python沙箱暴露丰富的Excel功能，并配备全面的工具集和精心设计的工具路由规则；(3)Domain-Spreadsheet基准数据集，涵盖金融和供应链管理等领域的专业评估任务。

Result: 通过全面实验验证，Spreadsheet-RL在通用和领域特定的电子表格任务上均显著提升了AI智能体性能，且展现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: Spreadsheet-RL显著提升了AI智能体在电子表格任务上的性能：Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507在SpreadsheetBench上的Pass@1从12.0%提升至23.4%，在Domain-Spreadsheet数据集上从8.4%提升至17.2%，展示了良好的泛化能力和实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Spreadsheet systems (e.g., Microsoft Excel, Google Sheets) play a central role in modern data-centric workflows. As AI agents grow increasingly capable of automating complex tasks, such as controlling computers and generating presentations, building an AI-driven spreadsheet agent has emerged as a promising research direction. Most existing spreadsheet agents rely on specialized prompting over general-purpose LLMs; while this design has potentials on simple spreadsheet operations, it struggles to manage the complex, multi-step workflows typical of real-world applications.
  We introduce Spreadsheet-RL, a reinforcement learning (RL) fine-tuning framework designed to train specialized spreadsheet agents within a realistic Microsoft Excel environment. Spreadsheet-RL features an automated pipeline for scalable collection of paired start-goal spreadsheets from online forums, as well as domain-specific evaluation tasks in areas such as finance and supply chain management, which we compile into the new Domain-Spreadsheet benchmark dataset. It also includes a Spreadsheet Gym environment designed for multi-turn RL: Spreadsheet Gym exposes extensive Excel functionality through a Python sandbox, along with a refined harness that incorporates a comprehensive tool set and carefully designed tool-routing rules for spreadsheet tasks. Through comprehensive experiments, we show that Spreadsheet-RL substantially enhances AI agent's performance on both general and domain-specific spreadsheet tasks: it improves Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507's Pass@1 on SpreadsheetBench from 12.0% to 23.4%, and raises Pass@1 from 8.4% to 17.2% on our curated Domain-Spreadsheet dataset. These results highlight Spreadsheet-RL's strong potential for generalization and real-world adoption in spreadsheet automation, and broadly, its promise for advancing LLM-based interactions with data interfaces in everyday work.

</details>


### [128] [AtelierEval: Agentic Evaluation of Humans & LLMs as Text-to-Image Prompters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22645)
*Hanjun Luo,Zhimu Huang,Sylvia Chung,Yiran Wang,Yingbin Jin,Jialin Li,Jiang Li,Xinfeng Li,Hanan Salam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出首个统一评估文本到图像系统上游提示器能力的新基准 AtelierEval，通过认知驱动的任务设计评估提示生成技能，并开发了 AtelierJudge 智能体评估器达到与人类专家高度一致（ρ=0.79）的评估效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试仅评估 T2I 模型，而上游提示器（人类或 MLLM）将用户意图转换为详细提示的能力完全未被测量，亟需一个统一框架来量化和诊断提示生成能力。

Method: 提出 AtelierEval 基准（360 个专家设计任务，基于认知理论涵盖三类任务）和 AtelierJudge 评估器（基于技能的记忆增强智能体评估器）。实验对比 8 个 MLLM 与 48 名人类用户在 4 个 T2I 后端上的表现。

Result: AtelierJudge 与人类专家的 Spearman 相关系数达 0.79，接近人类表现水平。

Conclusion: 研究证明了模仿优于规划的策略，并倡导未来提示器应采用图像增强方向。开源的 AtelierEval 和 AtelierJudge 为提示优化和 T2I 系统评估提供了可靠的诊断工具和可扩展的评估方法。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) systems increasingly rely on upstream prompters, either humans or multimodal large language models (MLLMs), to translate user intent into detailed prompts. Yet current benchmarks fix the prompt and only evaluate T2I models, leaving the prompting proficiency of this upstream component entirely unmeasured. We introduce AtelierEval, the first unified benchmark that quantifies prompting proficiency across 360 expert-crafted tasks. Grounded in a cognitive view, it spans three task categories and instantiates tasks using a taxonomy of real-world challenges, with a dual interface for both humans and MLLMs. To enable scalable and reliable evaluation, we propose AtelierJudge, a skill-based, memory-augmented agentic evaluator. It produces subjective and objective scores for prompt-image pairs, achieving a Spearman correlation of 0.79 with human experts, approaching human performance. Extensive experiments benchmark 8 MLLMs against 48 human users across 4 T2I backends, validate AtelierEval as a robust diagnostic tool, and reveal the superiority of mimicry over planning, advocating for an image-augmented direction for future prompters. Our work is released to support future research.

</details>


### [129] [Claw AI Lab: An Autonomous Multi-Agent Research Team](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22662)
*Fan Wu,Cheng Chen,Zhenshan Tan,Taiyu Zhang,Xinzhen Xu,Yanyu Qian,Dingcheng Gao,Lanyun Zhu,Qi Zhu,Yi Tan,Deyi Ji,Guosheng Lin,Tianrun Chen,Deheng Ye,Fayao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Claw AI Lab transforms autonomous research from rigid single-agent pipelines into an interactive AI laboratory with customizable research teams, real-time monitoring, and a novel code execution harness that improves experimental integrity and result faithfulness.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current automated research operates as a hidden "prompt-to-paper pipeline," limiting steerability, transparency, and reliability in autonomous AI-driven scientific discovery.

Method: The platform enables instantiating customizable research teams from a single prompt, featuring collaborative workflows, real-time monitoring, artifact inspection, and rollback/resume control via a unified dashboard. Key innovation is the Claw-Code Harness that connects local codebases/datasets/checkpoints to runnable experiments and feeds execution artifacts back into the research loop.

Result: Internal evaluation on five AI research case studies shows Claw AI Lab is consistently preferred by AI expert judges on idea novelty, experiment completeness, and paper presentation quality compared to AutoResearchClaw baseline.

Conclusion: Claw AI Lab represents an early step toward a new paradigm: autonomous research as usable, interactive, and reliability-aware scientific infrastructure.

Abstract: We present Claw AI Lab, a lab-native autonomous research platform that advances automated research from a hidden prompt-to-paper pipeline into an interactive AI laboratory. Rather than centering the system around a single agent or a fixed serial workflow, we allow users to instantiate a full research team from one prompt, with customizable roles, collaborative workflows, real-time monitoring, artifact inspection, and rollback/resume control through a unified dashboard. The platform also supports distinct research modes for exploration, multi-agent discussion, and reproduction, making autonomous research substantially more steerable and laboratory-like in practice. A key practical contribution of Claw AI Lab lies in its Claw-Code Harness, which connects local codebases, datasets, and checkpoints to runnable experiments and feeds execution artifacts back into the research loop. As a result, the harness improves not only execution integration, but also experimental completion and result integrity: experiments are easier to inspect, iterate on, and faithfully transfer into final papers, reducing common failure modes such as partial runs and malformed result reporting. In our internal evaluation on five AI research case studies, using AutoResearchClaw as the baseline, Claw AI Lab is consistently preferred by AI expert judges on idea novelty, experiment completeness, and paper presentation quality. We view Claw AI Lab as an early step toward a new paradigm: autonomous research as usable, interactive, and reliability-aware scientific infrastructure.

</details>


### [130] [Is Capability a Liability? More Capable Language Models Make Worse Forecasts When It Matters Most](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22672)
*Nick Merrill,Jaeho Lee,Ezra Karger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: More capable LLMs produce worse forecasts on superlinear growth/tail risk problems due to aggressive upper-tail extrapolations. Current benchmarks mask this via single-threshold metrics; tail-inclusive scoring reveals inverse scaling.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs exhibit inverse scaling on forecasting tasks involving superlinear growth and tail risk of regime change—common in finance and epidemiology. More capable models produce worse distributional forecasts on these tasks.

Method: Developed FBSim benchmark (contamination-free), tested on synthetic SIR epidemics, and replicated on real-world datasets (COVID-19, measles, housing markets, hyperinflation). Performed per-quantile decomposition and within-family study of Llama-3.1.

Result: Inverse scaling concentrates at the upper tail; more capable models shift upper tails upward tracking aggressive extrapolations. Both model scale and post-training independently contribute. Single-threshold metrics miss this effect; tail-inclusive scoring reverses the capability-accuracy relationship sign.

Conclusion: LLM forecasting evaluations should use continuous (unbounded) measures alongside bounded binary threshold metrics. The inverse scaling effect is real but currently masked by common evaluation practices.

Abstract: We document inverse scaling in LLMs on forecasting problems whose underlying time series exhibit superlinear growth and tail risk of regime change, a structure common in finance and epidemiology. On these tasks, more capable models produce worse distributional forecasts. The pattern appears on ForecastBench-Sim (FBSim), a contamination-free, simulated-world benchmark we release, in forecasting synthetic SIR epidemics with a matched linear control, and replicates in real-world datasets on COVID-19, measles, housing markets, and hyperinflation. A per-quantile decomposition shows the failure concentrates at the upper tail, which more capable models shift upward to track aggressive extrapolations of growth, while the lower tail stays put. A within-family study of Llama-3.1 shows that both model scale and post-training independently contribute to this effect. Domain knowledge does not reliably rescue calibration. This inverse scaling does not appear on single-threshold metrics common in LLM forecasting benchmarks, reversing the sign of the capability--accuracy relationship on identical outputs. Single-threshold scoring at conventional cutoffs misses the upper-tail cost; tail-inclusive scoring reverses the sign of the capability--accuracy relationship on the same outputs. We recommend that LLM forecasting evaluations use continuous (and unbounded) measures of accuracy alongside bounded binary threshold metrics.

</details>


### [131] [Forecasting Scientific Progress with Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22681)
*Sean Wu,Pan Lu,Yupeng Chen,Jonathan Bragg,Yutaro Yamada,Peter Clark,David Clifton,Philip Torr,James Zou,Junchi Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过CUSP基准评估前沿AI模型预测科学进步的能力，发现当前模型虽能识别研究方向的可行性，但无法可靠预测科学突破是否实现及发生时间，且存在系统性过度自信和信息价值错配问题，表明AI作为科学预测工具仍有根本性局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索当前AI系统是否具备预测科学进步的能力，这是一个尚未被系统研究的关键问题，对理解AI在科学发现中的真正潜力至关重要。

Method: 构建CUSP基准测试，包含4,760个跨学科科学事件，从可行性评估、机制推理、生成式解决方案设计和时序预测四个维度评估AI系统的科学预测能力。通过控制信息访问条件（预截断知识、全信息设置）进行对照实验，并分析训练截断前后的事件表现差异、引用量与性能关系。

Result: 前沿模型在CUSP基准上表现不佳：虽能从竞争性候选中识别合理研究方向，但无法可靠预测科学突破是否实现及发生时间；性能存在显著领域异质性，AI领域时序可预测性高于生物、化学、物理；预截断/后截断事件性能差异不显著，表明主要局限并非源于训练数据知识暴露；额外预截断知识有改善但无法弥合与全信息设置的差距，高引用进展差距更大；模型存在系统性过度自信和强响应偏差。

Conclusion: 当前AI系统在科学进步预测方面存在根本性局限：无法可靠预测科学突破是否会发生及何时发生；额外知识仅能部分改善性能，高引用进展的差距更明显；模型表现出系统性过度自信和响应偏差，不确定性估计不可靠。知识获取与可靠预测之间存在本质鸿沟，未来进展预测的后验信息价值高于前瞻性预测。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is increasingly embedded in scientific discovery, yet whether it can anticipate scientific progress remains unclear. To study this question, we introduce a temporally grounded evaluation framework for forecasting scientific progress under controlled knowledge constraints. We present CUSP (Cutoff-conditioned Unseen Scientific Progress), a multi-disciplinary and event-level benchmark that evaluates scientific forecasting in AI systems through feasibility assessment, mechanistic reasoning, generative solution design, and temporal prediction. Across 4,760 scientific events, we observe systematic and domain-dependent limitations in current frontier models. While models can identify plausible research directions from competing candidates, they fail to reliably predict whether scientific advances will be realized and systematically misestimate when they will occur. Performance is highly heterogeneous across domains, with the timing of AI progress more predictable than advances in biology, chemistry, and physics. Performance is largely insensitive to whether events occur before or after the training cutoff, suggesting these limitations cannot be explained solely by knowledge exposure in training data. Under controlled information access, additional pre-cutoff knowledge improves performance but does not close the gap to full-information settings, which becomes more pronounced for high-citation advances. Models also exhibit systematic overconfidence and strong response biases, indicating unreliable uncertainty estimation. Taken together, current AI systems fall short as predictive tools for scientific progress. Access to prior knowledge does not translate into reliable forecasting, and performance benefits more from post-event information than from forward-looking prediction.

</details>


### [132] [AMEL: Accumulated Message Effects on LLM Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22714)
*Sid-ali Temkit*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现LLM在进行自动化评估时会受到对话历史极性的系统性影响。负面历史比正面历史产生1.62倍的偏倚，效应在模型原本不确定的项目上更为显著，且不随上下文长度增加而加剧。模型规模扩大可缓解但无法消除这一偏倚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM被广泛用于代码审查、内容审核或输出评分等自动化评估任务，一个关键问题浮现：当多个评估项目通过同一对话处理时，先前对话历史的极性（正面或负面评价）是否会系统性地影响模型对后续项目的判断？这是一个关乎评估可靠性的基础性问题。

Method: 研究者在11个模型（来自OpenAI、Anthropic、Google及四个开源模型）上进行了75,898次API调用实验。实验设计包括将相同的测试项目以三种形式呈现：独立呈现、跟随正面评价历史、以及跟随负面评价历史。通过计算效应量（d值）、进行t检验和分析相关性来量化偏倚程度。

Result: 主要发现包括：（1）模型整体向对话的主导极性偏移（d=-0.17, p<10^-46）；（2）效应集中于模型原本不确定的项目（高熵项d=-0.34 vs 确定项d=-0.15）；（3）偏倚程度不随上下文长度增加（5轮与50轮产生相同偏移）；（4）存在负性偏倚不对称性，负面历史产生的偏倚是正面历史的1.62倍；（5）模型缩放有所改善但未能消除影响（Anthropic系列：Haiku -0.22至Opus -0.17；OpenAI系列：Nano -0.34至GPT-5.2 -0.17）。后续实验进一步表明：token概率分布连续变化而非阈值跳跃；负性不对称同时存在于token级和语义层面；位置无关紧要。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了LLM作为自动化评估器时的系统性偏见问题。虽然扩展模型规模有所帮助，但仍无法根除这种影响。对于评估流程，最简单的解决方案是对每个评估项使用新的上下文；当批处理不可避免时，应平衡历史记录中正负评价的比例。

Abstract: Large language models are routinely used as automated evaluators: to review code, moderate content, or score outputs, often with many items passing through one conversation. We ask whether the polarity of prior conversation history biases subsequent judgments, an effect we call the accumulated message effect on LLM judgments (AMEL). Across 75,898 API calls to 11 models from 4 providers (OpenAI, Anthropic, Google, and four open-source models), we present identical test items in isolation or following histories saturated with predominantly positive or negative evaluations.
  Models shift toward the conversation's prevailing polarity (d = -0.17, p < 10^-46). The effect concentrates on items where the model is genuinely uncertain at baseline (d = -0.34 for high-entropy items, vs d = -0.15 when the baseline is deterministic). Bias does not grow with context length: 5 prior turns and 50 produce the same shift (Spearman |r| < 0.01; OLS slope p = 0.80). And there is a negativity asymmetry: paired per item, negative histories induce 1.62x more bias than positive (t = 13.46, p < 10^-39, n = 2,481). Scaling helps but does not solve it (Anthropic: Haiku -0.22 to Opus -0.17; OpenAI: Nano -0.34 to GPT-5.2 -0.17).
  Three follow-ups narrow the mechanism. The token probability distribution shifts continuously, not at a threshold. The negativity asymmetry has both token-level and semantic components, though attributing the balance is exploratory at our sample sizes. Position does not matter: five biased turns anywhere in a 50-turn history produce the same shift. The simplest fix for evaluation pipelines is a fresh context per item; when batching is unavoidable, balancing the history helps.

</details>


### [133] [Parametric Modular Answer Set Programs Made Declarative](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22716)
*Jorge Fandinno,Yuliya Lierler,Torsten Schaub*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了一阶回答集编程(ASP)中的模块化概念，引入了参数化模块化逻辑程序形式化体系，该体系支持带参数的子程序定义和内涵语句，并能够捕获具有集体控制功能的clingo程序语义，为模块化ASP提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to explore and formalize the concept of modularity in first-order answer set programming (ASP).

Method: The authors introduce parametric modular logic programs, a formalism allowing subprogram definitions with parameters and intensionality statements.

Result: The proposed parametric modular logic programs formalism is demonstrated to capture the semantics of clingo-programs with collective control, which enables structuring and instantiating subprograms.

Conclusion: The paper provides theoretical foundations for modular ASP and connects modular approaches to traditional non-modular ASP.

Abstract: In this paper, we explore the concept of modularity in first-order answer set programming (ASP). We introduce a new formalism called parametric modular logic programs, which allows defining subprograms with parameters and intensionality statements. We demonstrate how this formalism can capture the semantics of clingo-programs with collective control, a feature that enables structuring and instantiating subprograms. We provide theoretical foundations for modular ASP, illustrate its usefulness, and connect to traditional non-modular ASP.

</details>


### [134] [Beyond Acoustic Emotion Recognition: Multimodal Pathos Analysis in Political Speech Using LLM-Based and Acoustic Emotion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22732)
*Juergen Dietrich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究验证了声学情感识别模型无法有效替代政治演讲Pathos维度分析，LLM多模态分析显著优于纯声学方法，标准SER语料库生态有效性存疑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pathos（情感说服力）是政治演讲分析的关键维度，但传统声学情感识别（SER）模型能否有效捕捉政治语境下的情感语义尚不明确，且现有SER基准语料库（如EMO-DB）的生态有效性未被系统验证。

Method: 使用邦巴斯基（Banaszak）德国联邦议院演讲（51段，245秒）作为案例研究；采用三种分析模态对比：（1）emotion2vec_plus_large声学模型+Russell环形模型投影；（2）Gemini 2.5 Flash多模态LLM（音频+转录文本）；（3）TRUST-Pathos三辩护人LLM监督集成；通过Spearman秩相关评估各模态与TRUST-Pathos的一致性；另对EMO-DB进行系统性质量评估。

Result: Gemini Valence与TRUST-Pathos强正相关（rho=+0.664, p<0.001），而emotion2vec Valence无显著相关（rho=+0.097, p=0.499）；EMO-DB存在表演性语音、文化偏差和类别不兼容问题。

Conclusion: LLM多模态分析在政治情感语义捕捉上优于纯声学模型，声学特征仅对底层唤醒度估计有参考价值；标准SER基准语料库存在表演性偏差、文化偏差和类别不兼容问题；未来将扩展至视频分析（面部表情、视线追踪）。

Abstract: We investigate whether acoustic emotion recognition models can serve as proxies for the Pathos dimension in political speech analysis, as operationalised by the TRUST multi-agent large language model (LLM) pipeline. Using a Bundestag plenary speech by Felix Banaszak (51 segments, 245 s) as a case study, we compare three analysis modalities: (1) emotion2vec_plus_large, an acoustic speech emotion recognition (SER) model whose continuous Arousal and Valence values are derived via post-hoc Russell Circumplex projection; (2) Gemini 2.5 Flash, an LLM analysing the full speech audio together with its transcript in an open-ended, context-aware fashion; and (3) TRUST-Pathos scores from a three-advocate LLM supervisor ensemble. Spearman rank correlations reveal that Gemini Valence correlates strongly with TRUST-Pathos (rho = +0.664, p < 0.001), whereas emotion2vec Valence does not (rho = +0.097, p = 0.499). We further demonstrate, via a systematic quality evaluation of the Berlin Database of Emotional Speech (EMO-DB) using Gemini in an open-ended annotation paradigm, that standard SER benchmark corpora suffer from acted speech, cultural bias, and category incompatibility. Our results suggest that LLM-based multimodal analysis captures semantically defined political emotion substantially better than acoustic models alone, while acoustic features remain informative for low-level Arousal estimation. Future work will extend this approach to video-based analysis incorporating facial expression and gaze.

</details>


### [135] [Towards a General Intelligence and Interface for Wearable Health Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22759)
*Girish Narayanswamy,Maxwell A. Xu,A. Ali Heydari,Samy Abdel-Ghaffar,Marius Guerard,Kara Vaillancourt,Zhihan Zhang,Jake Garrison,Levi Albuquerque,Dimitris Spathis,Hong Yu,Hamid Palangi,Xuhai "Orson" Xu,David G. T. Barrett,Joseph Breda,Jed McGiffin,Yubin Kim,Yuwei Zhang,Naghmeh Rezaei,Samuel Solomon,Karan Ahuja,Tim Althoff,Jake Sunshine,Ming-Zher Poh,Benjamin Yetton,Ari Winbush,Nicholas B. Allen,James M. Rehg,Isaac Galatzer-Levy,Yun Liu,John Hernandez,Anupam Pathak,Conor Heneghan,Yuzhe Yang,Ahmed A. Metwally,Pushmeet Kohli,Mark Malhotra,Shwetak Patel,Xin Liu,Daniel McDuff*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出首个基于万亿分钟级无标签数据预训练的可穿戴健康基础模型(PHG),通过大规模自监督学习将多模态传感器信号转化为通用健康表征,在35项健康预测任务上验证了缩放定律,并引入LLM智能体自动化下游模型设计,最终构建经1860名临床医生验证的个人健康助手。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴传感器虽能捕获丰富数据,但将低层传感器信号转化为高层健康状态面临双重挑战:个体间的表型多样性和基线差异导致泛化困难;高质量标注数据稀缺且标注成本高昂。

Method: (1)基于十亿分钟级无标签传感器信号对五百万参与者进行大规模预训练;(2)构建包含35项任务的评测体系验证缩放定律;(3)部署LLM智能体课堂自主搜索下游预测头;(4)通过1860名临床医生评分验证个人健康助手的安全性和有效性。

Result: 模型在心血管、代谢、睡眠、心理健康等多个领域35项预测任务上系统性提升性能;实现了标签高效的少样本学习,可鲁棒估计日常健康指标;LLM智能体随模型容量增加性能持续提升;临床医生评分验证了个人健康助手在相关性、情境感知和安全性方面的优势。

Conclusion: 提出的PHG模型成功突破了可穿戴健康数据的标注瓶颈,通过万亿分钟级预训练实现35项健康预测任务的先进性能,并通过LLM智能体自动化模型搜索和临床验证展示出实际应用价值。

Abstract: While ubiquitous wearable sensors capture a wealth of behavioral and physiological information, effectively transforming these signals into personalized health insights is challenging. Specifically, converting low-level sensor data into representations capable of characterizing higher-level states is difficult due to high phenotypic diversity and variation in individual baseline health, physiology, and lifestyle factors. Moreover, collecting wearable data paired with health outcome annotations is laborious and expensive, and retrospective annotation remains practically unfeasible, contributing to a scarcity of data with high-quality labels. To overcome these limitations, we propose a foundation model for wearable health that is pretrained on more than one trillion minutes of unlabeled sensor signals drawn from a large cohort of five million participants. We demonstrate that the joint scaling of model capacity and pretraining data volume leads to systematic improvements in performance, as evaluated on a diverse set of 35 health prediction tasks, spanning cardiovascular, metabolic, sleep, and mental health, as well as lifestyle choices and demographic factors. We find that this population scale representation unlocks label-efficient few-shot learning and generative capabilities for robust daily metric estimation. To further leverage this learned representation, we deploy a classroom of LLM agents to autonomously search the space of downstream predictive heads built on the model embeddings, showing broad performance improvements that increase with LLM model capacity. Finally, we show how integrating these downstream predictors into a Personal Health Agent can support model responses that are more relevant, contextually aware, and safe, and we validate this via 1,860 ratings from a cohort of clinicians.

</details>


### [136] [Advancing Mathematics Research with AI-Driven Formal Proof Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22763)
*George Tsoukalas,Anton Kovsharov,Sergey Shirobokov,Anja Surina,Moritz Firsching,Gergely Bérczi,Francisco J. R. Ruiz,Arun Suggala,Adam Zsolt Wagner,Eric Wieser,Lei Yu,Aja Huang,Miklós Z. Horváth,Andrew Ferrauiolo,Henryk Michalewski,Codrut Grosu,Thomas Hubert,Matej Balog,Pushmeet Kohli,Swarat Chaudhuri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly excel at mathematical reasoning, but their unreliability limits their utility in mathematics research. A mitigation is using LLMs to generate formal proofs in languages like Lean. We perform the first large-scale evaluation of this method's ability to solve open problems. Our most capable agent autonomously resolved 9 of 353 open Erdős problems at the per-problem cost of a few hundred dollars, proved 44/492 OEIS conjectures, and is being deployed in combinatorics, optimization, graph theory, algebraic geometry, and quantum optics research. A basic agent alternating LLM-based generation with Lean-based verification replicated the Erdős successes but proved costlier on the hardest problems. These findings demonstrate the power of AI-aided formal proof search and shed light on the agent designs that enable it.

</details>


### [137] [Gated DeltaNet-2: Decoupling Erase and Write in Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22791)
*Ali Hatamizadeh,Yejin Choi,Jan Kautz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Gated DeltaNet-2通过将Delta-rule模型中耦合的擦除与写入门解耦为独立的通道级门控（b_t和w_t），解决了现有模型的标量绑定限制，在保持线性时间复杂度和常数内存的同时，显著提升了长上下文任务（尤其是多键检索）性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前Delta-rule类模型（如Gated DeltaNet和KDA）共有的核心限制是：一个标量门同时控制两件不同的事——Key侧的旧内容擦除强度和Value侧的新内容写入强度，这种标量绑定（scalar tie）限制了模型对压缩记忆的精细编辑能力。

Method: 核心改进是将KDA中单一的通道级衰减标量门拆分为两个独立参数：通道级擦除门b_t和通道级写入门w_t。此外，论文还给出了快速权重更新视图和分块WY算法（将通道级衰减吸收为非对称擦除因子），以及保持高效并行训练的门控反向传播。

Result: 1.3B参数规模下，在100B FineWeb-Edu tokens训练后，Gated DeltaNet-2在语言建模、常识推理和检索任务上均超越了Mamba-2、Gated DeltaNet、KDA及Mamba-3变体；在长上下文RULER needle-in-a-haystack基准上优势最为显著。

Conclusion: Gated DeltaNet-2通过将擦除和写入操作解耦为独立的通道级门控，在保持高效并行训练的同时，在长上下文任务上展现出显著优势，尤其在多键检索设置和needle-in-a-haystack基准测试中表现突出。

Abstract: Linear attention replaces the unbounded cache of softmax attention with a fixed-size recurrent state, reducing sequence mixing to linear time and decoding to constant memory. The hard part is not just what to forget, but how to edit this compressed memory without scrambling existing associations. Delta-rule models subtract the current read before writing a new value, and Kimi Delta Attention (KDA) sharpens forgetting with channel-wise decay. But the active edit still uses a single scalar gate to control two different things: how much old content to erase on the key side and how much new content to commit on the value side. We introduce Gated DeltaNet-2, which generalizes both Gated DeltaNet and KDA by inheriting adaptive forgetting and channel-wise decay while addressing their shared limitation, the scalar tie between erasing and writing. Gated Delta Rule-2 separates these roles with a channel-wise erase gate b_t and a channel-wise write gate w_t, reducing to KDA when both gates collapse to the same scalar and to Gated DeltaNet when the decay also collapses. We derive a fast-weight update view, a chunkwise WY algorithm with channel-wise decay absorbed into asymmetric erase factors, and a gate-aware backward pass that preserves efficient parallel training. At 1.3B parameters trained on 100B FineWeb-Edu tokens, Gated DeltaNet-2 achieves the strongest overall results among Mamba-2, Gated DeltaNet, KDA, and Mamba-3 variants across language modeling, commonsense reasoning, and retrieval. Its advantage is most pronounced on long-context RULER needle-in-a-haystack benchmarks, where it improves the evaluated multi-key retrieval setting and remains strong in both recurrent and hybrid settings. Code is available at https://github.com/NVlabs/GatedDeltaNet-2.

</details>


### [138] [MOSS: Self-Evolution through Source-Level Rewriting in Autonomous Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22794)
*Qianshu Cai,Yonggang Zhang,Xianzhang Jia,Wei Xue,Jun Song,Xinmei Tian,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MOSS是一个源代码级自进化系统，能够在生产智能体基板上自主重写代码实现持续改进，相比仅修改文本配置的方式具有图灵完备性覆盖、更强确定性和抗长上下文漂移能力，在OpenClaw基准上实现单周期显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自主智能体系统在部署后完全静态，无法从用户交互中学习改进；现有自进化方案仅局限于文本层（技能文件、提示配置、记忆模式、工作流图），而路由逻辑、钩子排序、状态不变量和调度等核心机制存在于代码层，导致这类结构性故障无法被文本层触及和修复。

Method: 采用确定性多阶段流水线架构：1) 自动收集生产故障证据作为进化锚点；2) 通过可插拔的外部编码智能体CLI执行代码修改，MOSS仅保留阶段编排和判决权；3) 在临时试运行工作节点中重放故障批次验证候选代码；4) 通过用户授权的容器原地替换进行部署，并配备健康探针触发的回滚机制。

Result: 在OpenClaw基准的四任务测试中，MOSS在无需人工干预的单次迭代中将平均评分从0.25提升至0.61。

Conclusion: MOSS实现了生产级智能体在源代码级别的自主进化，在OpenClaw基准上实现了单周期分数从0.25到0.61的显著提升，证明了源码级适配相比文本可编辑方案具有更强的泛化能力和稳定性。

Abstract: Autonomous agentic systems are largely static after deployment: they do not learn from user interactions, and recurring failures persist until the next human-driven update ships a fix. Self-evolving agents have emerged in response, but all confine evolution to text-mutable artifacts -- skill files, prompt configurations, memory schemas, workflow graphs -- and leave the agent harness untouched. Since routing, hook ordering, state invariants, and dispatch live in code rather than in any text artifact, an entire class of structural failure is physically unreachable from the text layer. We argue that source-level adaptation is a fundamentally more general medium: it is Turing-complete, a strict superset of every text-mutable scope, takes effect deterministically rather than through base-model compliance, and does not erode under long-context drift. We present MOSS, a system that performs self-rewriting at the source level on production agentic substrates. Each evolution is anchored to an automatically curated batch of production-failure evidence and proceeds through a deterministic multi-stage pipeline; code modification is delegated to a pluggable external coding-agent CLI while MOSS retains stage ordering and verdicts. Candidates are verified by replaying the batch against the candidate image in ephemeral trial workers, then promoted via user-consent-gated, in-place container swap with health-probe-gated rollback. On OpenClaw, MOSS lifts a four-task mean grader score from 0.25 to 0.61 in a single cycle without human intervention.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [139] [Requirements Perception Gap across Stakeholders: A Comparative Survey of Aged Care Digital Health Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21495)
*Yuqing Xiao,John Grundy,Anuradha Madugalla,Elizabeth Manias*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过混合方法调查249名老年人、护理人员和开发人员，发现老年护理数字健康软件存在显著的需求缺口：开发人员高估用户对高级功能的满意度，而低估核心非功能需求（如易用性）的重要性；老年人和护理人员则更重视简洁性和可靠性而非功能密度。研究结果可用于指导未来共创设计、产品决策及隐私设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前老年护理数字健康软件存在“需求缺口”，软件开发人员对用户需求的理解与实际用户满意度之间存在显著偏差，亟需系统性地探究多方利益相关者的差异化需求。

Method: 混合方法调查法，整合249名参与者的定量问卷与定性开放式回答，借助推断性统计比较满意度并通过主题分析处理质性数据。

Result: 识别出关键需求缺口：开发人员过度关注高级功能和非功能需求（如易用性、响应性）的用户满意度，而老年人和护理人员更重视简洁性和可靠性；明确了利益相关者需求在当前设计和使用期望上的趋同与分歧点。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了老年人、护理人员和开发人员在功能需求与非功能需求上的显著差异，开发人员需在共创设计中平衡高级功能与核心可用性，同时纳入隐私设计原则。

Abstract: We sought to explore and compare the perspectives of three key stakeholder groups: older adults, caregivers (formal health providers and informal caregivers), and digital health software developers on key functional and non-functional requirements. We conducted a survey, designed based on the findings from an existing systematic review, to gather and analyse data related to the three stakeholder groups' (dis)satisfaction with current aged care digital health software and their views on key future aged care software requirements. A mixed-methods survey approach integrated quantitative questionnaire data and qualitative open-ended responses from a total sample of 249, comprised of older adults (103), formal and informal caregivers (41), and software developers (105). Data analysis utilised a mixed methods approach, employing inferential statistics to compare group satisfaction levels and thematic analysis for qualitative open-ended responses. Our analysis reveals a significant "Requirements Gap". Software developers tend to prioritise advanced features and functional requirements, significantly overestimating user satisfaction with core NFRs such as ease of use and responsiveness. Conversely, developers were more critical of existing functional features compared to older adults and caregivers, who prioritised simplicity and reliability over feature density. By combining quantitative and qualitative analysis, we identified where stakeholder priorities align and where they diverge across functional and non-functional requirements in both the current designs they used and the future designs they desire. Our findings present a stakeholder gap analysis that can guide future co-design processes, near-term product decisions, and privacy-by-design recommendations in aged care digital health.

</details>


### [140] [Articulate but Wrong: Self-Review Failures in LLM-Based Code Modernization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21537)
*Gokul Chandra Purnachandra Reddy,Aditya Lolla,Harsha Sanku*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究发现LLM在将Python 2代码现代化为Python 3时，39.7%的语义陷阱snippet会导致行为改变，但31.7%被生成模型自身错误地认可为行为保留。漂移集中在数值语义相关snippet上，且与模型规模/价格无单调关系，表明这是任务结构性问题而非模型能力不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM被广泛用于代码迁移任务，一个关键问题浮现：当LLM现代化遗留代码时，它能否识别自身输出悄然改变了可观察行为？这关乎自动化代码迁移的安全可靠性。

Method: 在60条snippet的平衡语料库上对11个生产级LLM（来自7个不同家族）执行1980次真实现代化调用，使用类型严格的行为oracle评估每个输出，然后让每个模型判断自身输出是否保留行为。采用对比设计：区分需要真正现代化且存在语义陷阱的snippet与仅需表面修改的良性对照snippet。

Result: 语义保留漂移普遍存在且可明确分离：语义陷阱snippet漂移率39.7%，良性对照snippet仅7.0%（差异+32.7个百分点）；漂移集中在特定snippet上，不同模型间关于困难snippet的一致性高（平均Pearson r=0.52），少量核心数值语义snippet几乎对所有模型和所有提示措辞都失败；自审查不是可靠的安全网，31.7%的漂移案例被生成模型默默认可，且各模型自误率呈双峰分布（从0%到100%），部分模型明确识别出破坏其输出的Py2/Py3语义差异但仍声称行为保留；漂移率与模型能力和价格非单调相关，各模型漂移率从5.6%到46.7%不等，不跟随模型能力平滑变化。

Conclusion: 作者发布了所有代码、提示词、60条snippet语料库、行为oracle、输出提取器和原始模型输出，表明这是高度可复现的研究。核心建议是：代码现代化任务需要外部行为验证而非依赖模型自我审查，且该失败与模型规模/价格无单调关系，表明这是任务结构性问题而非模型能力问题。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly used to migrate legacy code to modern stacks. We ask a deceptively simple question: when an LLM modernizes legacy code, can the same model be relied upon to recognize when its own output silently changes observable behavior? We run 1,980 real modernization calls across 11 production LLMs from 7 distinct families on a balanced 60-snippet legacy-Python-2 corpus, evaluate every output with a type-strict behavioral oracle, and then ask each model to judge whether its own output preserves behavior. We report four findings.
