Abstract: Despite strong predictive results in the clinical machine learning literature, the translation of these models into bedside use remains limited by systems-level barriers: heterogeneous data representations, the absence of standardized deployment workflows, and a mismatch between research prototypes and the concurrency and latency requirements of hospital environments. We present the SepsisAI-Orchestrator, an open-source modular platform that addresses this deployment gap for early sepsis detection. The platform integrates HL7 FHIR-inspired Clinical Document Architecture (CDA) preprocessing, NoSQL storage, a containerized LightGBM classifier served via REST APIs, and a Streamlit clinical dashboard, orchestrated with Docker and Kubernetes. A previously validated LightGBM model (F1 0.87-0.94 on PhysioNet 2019) is reused without modification; the contribution lies in the surrounding infrastructure and its empirical characterization under load. Using k6 with 50-1000 concurrent virtual users, we find that replica count must be matched to the physical CPU thread count of the host: scaling from 3 to 12 replicas on a 12-thread CPU reduces p95 latency from 3.3s to 1.41s (57.3% reduction) and eliminates all request failures, while over-provisioning to 24 or 48 replicas degrades performance due to scheduler contention. To our knowledge this U-shaped scaling behavior has not been quantified previously for clinical AI inference workloads. We do not claim prospective clinical validation. Source code and deployment manifests are available at https://github.com/nucleusai/sepsisai-orchestrator.

</details>


### [173] [Memory-R2: Fair Credit Assignment for Long-Horizon Memory-Augmented LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21768)
*Sikuan Yan,Ahmed Bahloul,Ercong Nie,Susanna Schwarzmann,Riccardo Trivisonno,Volker Tresp,Yunpu Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Memory-R2框架,用于训练长时程记忆增强的LLM智能体。核心算法LoGo-GRPO结合局部和全局组相对优化,解决多会话RL中记忆导致轨迹不可比的问题。通过从同一中间记忆状态进行局部重 rollout 实现更公平的对比,并通过共享参数协同学习设计联合优化记忆形成与演化。渐进式课程从8-16-32会话逐步增加训练时域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Training memory-augmented LLM agents with RL in multi-session environments is challenging because memory turns past actions into part of future environment. Different rollouts create different intermediate memory states, making trajectory-level comparisons unfair and violating assumptions behind group-relative methods like GRPO. Trajectory-level rewards provide noisy or biased credit signals for long-horizon memory operations.

Method: LoGo-GRPO combines local and global group-relative optimization. Global objective preserves end-to-end learning from long-horizon trajectory-level rewards, while local rerollouts compare different memory-operation outcomes from the same intermediate memory state. Additionally, a shared-parameter co-learning design instantiates a fact extractor and memory manager from the same LLM backbone through role-specific prompts. Progressive curriculum training increases horizon from 8 to 16 to 32 sessions.

Result: Local rerollouts from the same intermediate memory state yield fairer group comparisons and more precise supervision for memory construction. Joint optimization of memory formation and evolution is achieved through shared-parameter co-learning.

Conclusion: Memory-R2 provides an effective training paradigm for memory-augmented LLM agents in long-horizon multi-session settings. The co-learning design with shared parameters between fact extractor and memory manager, combined with progressive curriculum training, stabilizes multi-step RL over long memory horizons.

Abstract: Memory-augmented LLM agents enable interactions that extend beyond finite context windows by storing, updating, and reusing information across sessions. However, training such agents with reinforcement learning in multi-session environments is challenging because memory turns the agent's past actions into part of its future environment. Once different rollouts write, update, or delete different memories, they no longer share the same intermediate memory state, making trajectory-level comparisons fundamentally unfair. This violates a key assumption behind group-relative methods such as GRPO, where rollouts are compared as if they were sampled from the same effective environment. Consequently, trajectory-level rewards provide noisy or biased credit signals for long-horizon memory operations. To address this challenge, we introduce Memory-R2, a training framework for long-horizon memory-augmented LLM agents. Its core algorithm, LoGo-GRPO, combines local and global group-relative optimization. The global objective preserves end-to-end learning from long-horizon trajectory-level rewards, while local rerollouts compare different memory-operation outcomes from the same intermediate memory state, yielding fairer group comparisons and more precise supervision for memory construction. Beyond credit assignment, Memory-R2 jointly optimizes memory formation and memory evolution with a shared-parameter co-learning design, where a fact extractor and a memory manager are instantiated from the same LLM backbone through role-specific prompts. To stabilize multi-step RL over long memory horizons, we adopt a progressive curriculum that increases the training horizon from 8 to 16 to 32 sessions. Together, these components provide an effective training paradigm for memory-augmented LLM agents in long-horizon multi-session settings.

</details>


### [174] [Harnesses for Inference-Time Alignment over Execution Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21516)
*Boyuan Wang,Bochao Li,Minghan Wang,Yuxin Tao,Fang Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过轨迹对齐的视角研究harness设计，将harness解耦为任务分解和引导执行两个机制，揭示了工作流粒度、重试预算和动作重加权如何影响性能极限，识别出过度分解、过度剪枝和执行幻觉三种失败模式，并证明部分结构化的harness优于完全结构化的工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型(LLM)智能体的推理时技术中，harness工程（任务分解与引导执行）虽能提升长期性能，但更精细的harness设计并不一定带来更好的效果——增加分解或引导有时反而会降低任务成功率。现有研究缺乏对这一现象的系统性理论解释。

Method: The authors model harnesses as two decoupled mechanisms: task decomposition (structuring tasks into sub-goals) and guided execution (reshaping local action distributions). They analyze how workflow granularity, retry budgets, and action reweighting affect performance limits through controlled experiments on synthetic tasks and real terminal agent benchmarks.

Result: 识别出三种具体失败模式：过度分解(over-decomposition)、过度剪枝(over-pruning)和执行幻觉(hallucinated execution)。实验验证了这些预测，发现部分结构化的harness（即仅指定初始步骤）相比完全结构化的工作流能达到更高的通过率。

Conclusion: Partial harnesses that specify only initial steps outperform fully structured workflows. This finding challenges the assumption that more elaborate harnesses are uniformly beneficial, offering a principled approach to harness design.

Abstract: Harness engineering has emerged as an important inference-time technique for large language model (LLM) agents, aiming to improve long-term performance through task decomposition and guided execution. However, more elaborate harnesses are not uniformly better: increasing decomposition or guidance can sometimes improve execution, but can also reduce final task success. We study harness design through the lens of inference-time trajectory alignment. This perspective separates harness into two mechanisms: task decomposition, which structures a task into sub-goals, and guided execution, which reshapes local action distributions during execution. This decomposition allows us to quantify how workflow granularity, retry budgets, and guidance-induced action reweighting shape the performance limits of harness design. It further reveals concrete failure modes, including over-decomposition, over-pruning, and hallucinated execution. We validate these predictions through controlled synthetic experiments and real terminal agent benchmarks. Inspired by the theory, we further show that effective harnesses can be partial: specifying only the initial steps and leaving the remaining execution to agent can achieve higher pass rate than fully structured workflows.

</details>


### [175] [Lumberjack: Better Differentially Private Random Forests through Heavy Hitter Detection in Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22756)
*Christian Janos Lebeda,David Erb,Tudor Cebere,Aurélien Bellet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Lumberjack是一种差分隐私随机森林算法，通过构建大型随机树并激进剪枝提升效用，其核心贡献是一个层级数据的DP重击者检测算法（误差O(√(log h))），实验表明该方法显著优于现有DP随机森林方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机森林在处理敏感表格数据中应用广泛，但现有差分隐私（DP）方案通常会导致性能严重下降，实际应用困难。

Method: Lumberjack通过构建大型随机决策树，然后应用激进的隐私保护剪枝策略（保留足够样本的节点）来提升效用。其核心是一个新颖的(ε,δ)-DP重击者检测算法，专为层级数据设计，误差仅为O_{ε,δ}(√(log h))。该算法的误差缩放特性允许使用比先前工作深得多的树结构，从而在隐私约束下获得更强的表达能力。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实验表明，Lumberjack始终优于现有DP随机森林方法，且在实用隐私预算下实现了隐私-效用权衡的实质性提升。

Conclusion: Lumberjack establishes a new state of the art for DP随机森林，显著缩小了隐私保护与模型效用之间的差距，表明经过精心设计的差分隐私随机森林算法具有重要研究价值和实际应用前景。

Abstract: Random forests are widely used in fields involving sensitive tabular data, but existing approaches to enforcing differential privacy (DP) typically degrade performance to the point of impracticality. In this paper, we introduce Lumberjack, a differentially private random forest algorithm that achieves substantially higher utility by constructing large random decision trees and then applying aggressive, privacy-preserving pruning to retain only sufficiently populated nodes. A key component of our approach is a novel $(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP heavy hitter detection algorithm for hierarchical data, whose error is $O_{\varepsilon,δ}(\sqrt{\log h})$ for trees of height $h$ and may be of independent interest. This favorable scaling enables the use of significantly deeper trees than in prior work, leading to improved expressiveness under privacy constraints. Our empirical evaluation on benchmark datasets shows that Lumberjack consistently outperforms prior DP random forest methods, establishing a new state of the art. In particular, our approach yields substantial improvements in the privacy-utility trade-off for practical privacy budgets. Our findings suggest that carefully designed DP random forests can close much of the utility gap, highlighting a promising and underexplored direction for future research.

</details>


### [176] [A Reproducible Log-Driven AutoML Framework for Interpretable Pipeline Optimization in Healthcare Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21528)
*Rui Huang,Lican Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出yvsoucom-iterkit框架，通过日志驱动的可复现AutoML系统分析18000+配置，发现性能由少量交互组件决定，数据增强和类别不平衡处理是关键因素，且组件间存在显著冗余，可通过聚焦高影响力组件实现高效优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 疾病风险预测面临异构特征、样本有限和严重类别不平衡三大挑战，亟需可复现且高效的自动化机器学习方案。

Method: 提出yvsoucom-iterkit框架，将流水线优化形式化为完全可复现的日志驱动配置级系统。每个流水线编码为可追溯的日志实体，支持组件归因、交互、相似性和跨种子鲁棒性分析。基于18000+配置的搜索空间分析，使用随机森林评估特征重要性，并采用RMS距离量化组件相似性。

Result: 搜索空间呈结构化且部分冗余，性能由少量交互组件主导。Pima数据集关键驱动因素：数据增强(0.454)、模型选择(0.198)、不平衡处理(0.101)；Stroke数据集：不平衡处理(0.406)占主导。组件相似性显示强冗余：特征选择变体RMS距离低(0.0252)，mixup与无增强接近(0.0279)，TomekLinks与无不平衡处理对齐(0.0325)，高斯噪声偏离较大(0.10)。

Conclusion: 有效的AutoML优化可聚焦于缩减的高影响力组件集。实验表明集成模型表现稳定优秀（Pima: Weighted-F1 0.89, Macro-F1 0.88; Stroke: Weighted-F1 0.94），但Stroke数据集因类别不平衡导致Macro-F1较低(0.67)。集成模型变异性(0.023-0.026)低于SVM，呈现出性能-稳健性权衡。

Abstract: Accurate and reproducible disease risk prediction remains challenging due to heterogeneous features, limited samples, and severe class imbalance. This study introduces yvsoucom-iterkit, a deterministic and log-driven automated machine learning framework that formulates pipeline optimization as a fully reproducible, configuration-level system. Each pipeline is encoded as a traceable log entity, enabling analysis of component attribution, interactions, similarity, and cross-seed robustness. Experiments on the Pima Indians Diabetes and Stroke datasets across more than 18,000 pipeline configurations reveal a structured and partially redundant search space, where performance is governed by a small subset of interacting components. Random Forest importance analysis identifies augmentation (0.454), model choice (0.198), and imbalance handling (0.101) as key drivers on Pima, while imbalance handling dominates Stroke (0.406). Component similarity analysis shows strong redundancy, with feature selection variants (biMax-biMean) exhibiting low RMS distance (0.0252), mixup closely matching no augmentation (0.0279), and TomekLinks aligning with no imbalance handling (0.0325), whereas Gaussian noise shows greater divergence from no augmentation (0.10). The framework achieves strong and stable performance using ensemble models (Weighted-F1 0.89, Macro-F1 0.88 on Pima; Weighted-F1 0.94 on Stroke), while Macro-F1 remains lower on Stroke (0.67) due to class imbalance. Cross-seed analysis reveals a performance-robustness trade-off, with ensembles showing lower variability (0.023-0.026) than SVM. These results indicate that effective AutoML optimization can focus on a reduced set of high-impact components.

</details>


### [177] [DualOptim+: Bridging Shared and Decoupled Optimizer States for Better Machine Unlearning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21539)
*Xuyang Zhong,Qizhang Li,Yiwen Guo,Chen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DualOptim+ is a novel optimization framework for improving machine unlearning in LLMs. It uses a dual-state architecture with a base state for shared representations and delta states for objective-specific residuals, enabling adaptive bridging between shared and decoupled states based on gradient conflicts. A quantized 8bit variant is also introduced for memory efficiency.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The paper aims to improve machine unlearning in large language models, which faces the challenge of balancing forgetting (removing unwanted knowledge) and retaining (preserving useful knowledge) objectives.

Method: DualOptim+ introduces a base state for capturing shared representations and delta states for preserving objective-specific residuals. The optimizer adaptively bridges shared and decoupled states based on the directional conflict between forgetting and retaining gradients. A quantized variant (8bit) is also proposed to reduce memory overhead.

Result: Extensive experiments across fictitious and real-world unlearning, safety alignment, and multi-task learning tasks demonstrate that DualOptim+ consistently achieves superior trade-offs between different objectives.

Conclusion: DualOptim+ achieves a superior trade-off between forgetting and retaining objectives through its dual-state architecture, with DualOptim+ 8bit offering memory-efficient performance. The framework consistently demonstrates effectiveness across unlearning, safety alignment, and multi-task learning tasks.

Abstract: We propose DualOptim+, a novel optimization framework for improving machine unlearning in large language models. It introduces a base state to capture common representations shared by forgetting and retaining objectives and delta states to preserve objective-specific residuals. This architecture allows the optimizer to adaptively bridge shared and decoupled states based on the directional conflict between forgetting and retaining gradients. We further introduce DualOptim+ 8bit, a quantized variant that reduces memory overhead without compromising performance. Extensive experiments across fictitious and real-world unlearning, safety alignment, and multi-task learning tasks demonstrate that DualOptim+ consistently achieves a superior trade-off between different objectives. Codes are available at https://github.com/CityU-MLO/DualOptimPlus.

</details>


### [178] [Provable Joint Decontamination for Benchmarking Multiple Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21543)
*Zhenlong Liu,Hao Zeng,Hongxin Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper addresses LLM benchmark contamination by proposing JECS, a conformal selection procedure that ensures fair cross-model comparison with provable global contamination rate control.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Benchmark data contamination inflates reported performance and undermines reliable cross-model comparisons when evaluation examples appear in training data.

Method: JECS computes per-model conformal p-values, aggregates them by per-item maximum, reconstructs a conservative envelope of the max-p null distribution from right-tail observations above a data-driven threshold, then applies the adaptive Benjamini-Hochberg procedure to select benchmarks.

Result: JECS achieves higher power than the max-p baseline while consistently maintaining the target global contamination rate control across various models and benchmarks.

Conclusion: JECS enables provable global contamination rate control while maintaining fair cross-model comparison.

Abstract: Benchmark data contamination has become a central challenge in LLM evaluation: when evaluation examples appear in the training data of one or more audited models, reported performance can be inflated and cross-model comparisons become unreliable. A broad line of training-data detection work designs scores to quantify how strongly a model memorizes a given data point, but these score-based methods lack theoretical guarantees. Recent conformal approaches provide provable false-identification control for a single model; however, applying them separately to each model can produce model-specific benchmarks, undermining fair comparison across models. In this work, we formalize multi-model benchmark decontamination as a joint selection problem and propose Joint Envelope Conformal Selection (JECS), a conformal procedure that enables global contamination rate (GCR) control under stated assumptions. Specifically, JECS computes per-model conformal p-values, aggregates them by the per-item maximum, and reconstructs a conservative envelope of the max-p null distribution from right-tail observations above a data-driven threshold. By applying the adaptive Benjamini-Hochberg (BH) procedure to the envelope-rescaled values, we select a benchmark with provable GCR control. Extensive experiments across various models and benchmarks demonstrate that JECS achieves higher power than the max-p baseline while consistently maintaining the target GCR control.

</details>


### [179] [Tabular foundation models for robust calibration of near-infrared chemical sensing data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21544)
*Robin Reiter,Denis Cornet,Fabien Michel,Lauriane Rouan,Gregory Beurier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了表格基础模型TabPFN在近红外光谱化学传感校准中的表现，在66个数据集上与多种经典模型对比，发现预处理优化的TabPFN在回归任务中表现最佳，分类任务中直接应用效果最好，但面对异常值和外推场景时性能下降，需要结合不确定性感知策略部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NIR光谱技术的实际应用面临高维共线性光谱、有限样本量、预处理依赖性、光谱异常值及校准域外推等挑战，需要开发能够应对这些问题的校准模型

Method: 在统一验证框架下，对66个NIR数据集（54个回归+12个分类任务）进行基准测试，将TabPFN与PLS/PLS-DA、Ridge、Catboost、CNN-1D进行对比；预处理和模型选择仅在校准数据上完成

Result: 回归任务中，经预处理优化的TabPFN获得最佳平均排名，显著优于PLS、CatBoost、原始TabPFN和CNN-1D，与Ridge统计可比；分类任务中，TabPFN直接应用于原始光谱取得最佳平均排名；在光谱异常值和外推样本场景下优势减弱，经典化学计量模型仍具竞争力

Conclusion: TabPFN作为表格基础模型可补充传统化学计量学工作流程，适用于中小型校准集场景；但需针对光谱异常值和外推样本场景开发专用的先验知识和不确定性感知部署策略

Abstract: Near-infrared spectroscopy is increasingly used as a rapid, non-destructive chemical sensing technology for the analysis of food, pharmaceutical, biological, and environmental samples. However, the practical deployment of NIR sensors still depends on calibration models able to handle high-dimensional, collinear spectra, limited sample sizes, preprocessing dependence, spectral outliers, and extrapolation beyond the calibration domain. Here, we evaluate whether tabular foundation models can provide a new calibration strategy for NIR chemical sensing. We benchmark TabPFN on 66 NIR datasets covering 54 regression and 12 classification tasks, and compare direct inference on raw spectra with preprocessing-optimized inference against PLS/PLS-DA, Ridge, Catboost, and one-dimensional convolutional neural networks. The study uses a unified validation framework in which preprocessing and model selection are performed exclusively on calibration data before external test evaluation. In regression, preprocessing-optimized TabPFN achieves the best overall average rank and significantly outperforms PLS, CatBoost, TabPFN on raw spectra, and CNN-1D, while remaining statistically comparable to Ridge. In classification, TabPFN applied directly to raw spectra provides the best average rank, with performance close to the optimized variant. Robustness analyses show that TabPFN provides strong average predictive performance but that its advantage decreases on spectral outliers and extrapolated samples, where classical chemometric models remain competitive. These results suggest that tabular foundation models can complement established chemometric workflows for NIR chemical sensing, especially in small- to medium-sized calibration settings, while highlighting the need for spectroscopy-specific priors and uncertainty-aware deployment strategies.

</details>


### [180] [Expectation Consistency Loss: Rethink Confidence Calibration under Covariate Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21552)
*Jinzong Dong,Zhaohui Jiang,Bo Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对协变量偏移场景下的分类模型置信度校准问题，提出期望一致性条件作为校准必要充分条件，并设计ECL损失函数实现兼容多种校准范式的无监督域适应校准，同时保证与ECE相当的样本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有置信度校准方法假设训练与测试数据独立同分布(i.i.d.)，在协变量偏移(covariate shift)场景下效果受限；此前方法存在类级校准或规范校准的局限，且依赖不稳定的密度比重要性加权（当密度比过大或无界时）。

Method: 1) 提出期望一致性条件(Expectation consistency condition)作为协变量偏移下置信度校准的必要充分条件；2) 基于该条件设计期望一致性损失(ECL)用于无监督域适应校准，支持规范校准、类级校准和top-label校准三种范式；3) 证明ECL损失的计算复杂度与ECE相当，并提供理论支撑的小批量可训练优化方案。

Result: 实验验证表明该方法在模拟和真实协变量偏移数据集上均有效。

Conclusion: 该研究为协变量偏移下的置信度校准提供了理论基础和实用方法。通过期望一致性条件揭示了协变量偏移与校准失败之间的非必然关系，并提出兼容多种校准范式的ECL损失函数，在保证可学习性的同时提升了校准鲁棒性，在模拟和真实数据集上验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Confidence calibration for classification models is vital in safety-critical decision-making scenarios and has received extensive attention. General confidence calibration methods assume training and test data are independent and identically distributed, limiting their effectiveness under covariate shifts. Previous calibration methods under covariate shift struggle with class-wise or canonical calibrations and often rely on unstable importance weighting when density ratios are large or unbounded. Given the above limitations, this paper rethinks confidence calibration under covariate shifts. First, we derive a necessary and sufficient condition for confidence calibration under covariate shifts, named Expectation consistency condition, which reveals covariate shifts do not necessarily lead to uncalibrated confidence and provides a weaker condition for confidence calibration than global covariate distribution alignment. Then, utilizing Expectation consistency condition, this paper proposes an unsupervised domain adaptation loss to calibrate confidence of the target domain, named Expectation consistency loss (ECL), which is compatible with canonical calibration, class-wise calibration, and top-label calibration. Third, we prove that computing ECL loss has the same sample complexity as Expected Calibration Error (ECE) and provide a theoretically grounded mini-batch trainable scheme for ECL loss. Finally, we validate the effectiveness of our method on both simulated and real-world covariate shift datasets.

</details>


### [181] [VeriScale: Adversarial Test-Suite Scaling for Verifiable Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22368)
*Yifan Bai,Xiaoyang Liu,Zihao Mou,Guihong Wang,Jian Yu,Shuhan Xie,Yantao Li,Yangyu Zhang,Jingwei Liang,Tao Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VeriScale是一种基于对抗性实现的基准测试构建框架，通过测试套件扩展和缩减两阶段方法提升LLM代码生成评估质量，在Verina基准上实例化后生成的VerinaPlus将测试套件扩展83倍并有效暴露模型弱点，VerinaLite则提供了轻量级14倍变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing benchmarks for evaluating LLMs in software engineering are limited by quantity and quality of positive/negative test cases, leading to overestimation of model capabilities in generating specifications and implementations.

Method: Two-stage framework consisting of: (1) Test-suite expansion using adversarial implementations to construct diverse and challenging test cases, and (2) Test-suite reduction to distill compact yet discriminative test suites. Instantiated on Verina benchmark.

Result: VerinaPlus expands original test suites by over 83× and exposes substantial model weaknesses through sharp score drops on SpecGen and CodeGen tasks across eight state-of-the-art LLMs. VerinaLite achieves 14× expansion while maintaining discriminative power at reduced evaluation cost.

Conclusion: VeriScale framework effectively improves benchmark quality for LLM evaluation in software engineering; VerinaPlus exposes significant model weaknesses through 83× expanded test suites, while VerinaLite offers a lightweight 14× variant maintaining discriminative power at reduced evaluation cost; code and enhanced benchmarks are publicly available.

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed for software engineering, constructing high-quality benchmarks is crucial for evaluating not just the functional correctness, but also the formal verifiability of generated code. However, existing benchmarks are limited by the quantity and quality of positive and negative test cases, leading to an overestimation of model capabilities in generating specifications and implementations. To address this, we propose VeriScale, a novel framework driven by the adversarial implementations. It consists of two stages: test-suite expansion to construct diverse and challenging test cases, and test-suite reduction to distill them into compact yet discriminative suites. While VeriScale is general, we instantiate it on Verina to construct VerinaPlus, which expands the original test suites by over 83$\times$, and VerinaLite, a lightweight 14$\times$ variant. Our experiments across eight state-of-the-art LLMs demonstrate that VerinaPlus exposes substantial model weaknesses hidden by the original benchmark, evidenced by sharp score drops on both SpecGen and CodeGen tasks, whereas VerinaLite maintains this discriminative power at a fraction of the evaluation cost. The enhanced benchmarks and source code are publicly available at https://github.com/XiaoyangLiu-sjtu/VeriScale.

</details>


### [182] [Beyond Single Slot: Joint Optimization for Multi-Slot Guaranteed Display Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21556)
*Zhaoqi Zhang,Jiaming Deng,Miao Xie,Linyou Cai,Qianlong Xie,Xingxing Wang,Siqiang Luo,Gao Cong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多槽GD广告分配问题，提出结合离线二部图匹配、合同轮盘机制和PV约束的联合优化框架，并在美团平台验证了显著的用户收益和收入效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 guaranteed display(GD) 广告分配方法多基于单槽假设，难以在多槽页面浏览场景下进行全局优化分配，存在槽级冗余、合同不平衡和曝光集中等挑战。

Method: 提出一种多槽GD分配的联合优化框架，将分配问题建模为离线二部图匹配问题，引入合同轮盘机制保障槽位独占性，并设置页面浏览量(PV)约束控制曝光集中度，同时设计了可扩展的分配优化算法以支持大规模部署。

Result: 在线A/B测试显示，在70%流量条件下，用户平均收入(ARPU)提升28.99%；双重差分(DID)分析表明合同稳定性得到改善。

Conclusion: 本研究提出了一种基于离线二部图匹配的多槽GD分配框架，通过轮盘机制和PV约束实现了流量精细化控制，在美团平台的在线A/B测试中验证了其在用户收益、平台收入效率和合同履约稳定性方面的显著提升，具有较强的工业落地价值。

Abstract: Guaranteed display advertising is crucial for platform monetization, yet existing methods often operate under a single-slot assumption, limiting their ability to optimize allocation across multi-slot page views. In this paper, we propose a novel joint optimization framework for multi-slot GD allocation, addressing key challenges such as slot-level redundancy, contract imbalance, and exposure concentration. Our approach formulates the allocation as an offline bipartite matching problem with a contract roulette mechanism for slot exclusivity and Page View constraints for impression control, and incorporates a scalable allocation optimization algorithm for efficient large-scale deployment. Extensive online tests on the Meituan advertising platform demonstrate that our method significantly improves merchant ROI, platform revenue efficiency, and contract fulfillment robustness. Specifically, online A/B tests show a 28.99% increase in Average Revenue Per User under 70% traffic, and DID analysis further indicates improved contract stability, demonstrating the strong applicability and effectiveness of our framework in real-world advertising deployments.

</details>


### [183] [Leveraging Self-Paced Curriculum Learning for Enhanced Modality Balance in Multimodal Conversational Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21565)
*Phuong-Anh Nguyen,The-Son Le,Duc-Trong Le,Cam-Van Thi Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于自步课程学习的即插即用框架SPCL用于多模态对话情感识别，通过双层难度测量器和动态学习调度器解决模态不平衡问题，在IEMOCAP和MELD数据集上验证了有效性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态情感识别任务中存在模态对齐问题和学习不平衡挑战，限制了多模态信息的有效利用。

Method: 提出基于自步课程学习(SPCL)的即插即用框架，包含双层难度测量器(话语层和对话层)和学习调度器。话语层评分建模细粒度模态特定难度，对话层评分捕捉更广泛的对话结构(情感依赖和模态一致性)。学习调度器根据难度评分动态引导从易到难的训练过程。

Result: 在IEMOCAP数据集上加权F1-score提升约+1.2%至+6.6%；在MELD数据集上提升达+10.4%。

Conclusion: SPCL作为轻量级即插即用模块，可有效集成到现有MERC架构中，在IEMOCAP和MELD数据集上均表现优异，验证了其有效性和可推广性。

Abstract: Multimodal Emotion Recognition in Conversations (MERC) is a crucial task for understanding human interactions, where multimodal approaches integrating language, facial expressions, and vocal tone have achieved significant progress. However, modality misalignment and imbalanced learning remain major challenges, limiting the effective utilization of multimodal information. To address this issue, we propose a plug-and-play framework based on Self-Paced Curriculum Learning (SPCL) for MERC. We introduce a dual-level Difficulty Measurer that captures both utterance-level and conversation-level challenges. The utterance-level score models fine-grained modality-specific difficulty, while the conversation-level score captures broader dialogue structures, including emotional dependencies and modality coherence. Based on these scores, the Learning Scheduler dynamically guides training from easier to more difficult instances. By integrating SPCL into existing MERC architectures, our method alleviates modality imbalance and improves model robustness. Extensive experiments on the IEMOCAP and MELD datasets demonstrate consistent improvements across different architectures and modality settings. On IEMOCAP, SPCL improves weighted F1-score by approximately +1.2% to +6.6% over baseline models, while on MELD, gains reach up to +10.4%. These results highlight the effectiveness and generalizability of SPCL as a lightweight plug-and-play module for multimodal emotion recognition.

</details>


### [184] [Calibration, Uncertainty Communication, and Deployment Readiness in CKD Risk Prediction: A Framework Evaluation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21566)
*Michael O. Eniolade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究评估了五种机器学习模型在CKD风险预测中的校准和不确定性量化性能。发现在内部测试集上所有模型达到近乎完美的辨别力（AUC=1.00），但这一性能未能迁移到外部数据（MIMIC-IV），AUROC降至随机水平左右，ECE从0.02升至0.7以上，conformal coverage从90%目标降至约22%。强调了在临床部署前必须在外部数据上验证校准稳定性和覆盖率的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 慢性肾病（CKD）机器学习风险预测模型虽然在内部测试集上表现出色，但校准和不确定性量化研究不足，临床医生缺乏可靠的概率输出准确性信息。

Method: 使用UCI CKD数据集（400例患者，62.5% CKD患病率）训练5个分类器（逻辑回归、随机森林、XGBoost、SVM with Platt scaling、高斯朴素贝叶斯），通过ECE、Brier Score和分 splits conformal prediction（目标90%边际覆盖率）评估校准质量和不确定性。使用MIMIC-IV演示队列（97例患者，23.7% CKD）进行分布压力测试。

Result: 内部测试集所有模型AUROC均达1.00，等渗重校准后ECE降至0.000-0.022。外部数据上AUROC降至0.48-0.58，ECE升至0.68-0.76， conformal coverage从0.80-0.98降至0.21-0.25（目标90%）。

Conclusion: 所有五个模型在内部测试集上达到AUROC 1.00，但在外部数据（MIMIC-IV）中性能大幅下降，AUROC降至0.48-0.58，ECE升至0.68-0.76， conformal coverage从0.80-0.98降至0.21-0.25。没有任何模型在部署就绪清单上超过4分（共16分）。结果表明：近完美的内部性能无法迁移；临床预测模型在部署前必须在外部数据上评估校准稳定性和conformal coverage。

Abstract: Machine learning models for chronic kidney disease (CKD) risk prediction often post strong discrimination scores on internal test sets. Calibration and uncertainty quantification get far less attention, leaving clinicians without reliable information about whether the probability outputs are accurate.
  We trained five classifiers on the UCI CKD dataset (400 patients, 62.5% CKD prevalence): logistic regression, random forest, XGBoost, SVM with Platt scaling, and Gaussian naive Bayes. We evaluated each across calibration quality, conformal prediction coverage, and an eight-criterion deployment readiness framework. A distributional stress-test applied the best-calibrated variant of each model to the open-access MIMIC-IV demo cohort (97 patients, 23.7% CKD) to assess behaviour under prevalence shift and feature missingness. We measured calibration before and after Platt scaling and isotonic regression using Expected Calibration Error and Brier Score, and quantified uncertainty through split conformal prediction targeting 90% marginal coverage.
  All five models reached AUROC 1.00 on the UCI test set. Isotonic recalibration reduced internal ECE to 0.000-0.022. On MIMIC-IV, AUROC fell to 0.48-0.58, ECE rose to 0.68-0.76, and conformal coverage dropped from 0.80-0.98 to 0.21-0.25 against a 90% target. No model scored above 4 out of 16 on the deployment readiness checklist.
  Near-perfect internal performance did not transfer. Calibration stability and conformal coverage should be evaluated on external data before any clinical prediction model moves toward deployment.

</details>


### [185] [ConTact: Contact-First Antibody CDR Design via Explicit Interface Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21600)
*Mansoor Ahmed,Spencer VonBank,Nadeem Taj,Sujin Lee,Naila Jan,Murray Patterson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 ConTact，一种将 CDR 设计显式分解为表面互补学习、接触预测和门控序列生成的级联架构，通过距离偏置交叉注意力和接触加权损失解决现有方法信号稀释问题，在 CHIMERA-Bench 上取得最优结构质量和表位感知性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算抗体 CDR 设计方法将两个本质不同的子问题混为一谈：CDR 接触位点识别和氨基酸选择。这种混淆导致模型不得不通过统一的消息传递机制隐式学习接触推理，使抗原信号被均匀分散到所有位置，削弱了关键binding位置的梯度信号。

Method: 该研究提出了一种名为 ConTact 的级联三阶段架构：1）学习表面互补指纹特征；2）预测 CDR-抗原接触区域；3）将接触门控的抗原特征注入序列头部。同时引入了距离偏置的交叉注意力模块（编码几何先验）和接触加权交叉熵损失函数（聚焦binding-critical位置）。

Result: 在 CHIMERA-Bench 基准数据集上，ConTact 实现了最佳结构质量（相比次优基线 RMSD 提升 7%）、最佳表位感知能力（相比 GNN 基线 F1 分数提升 10%）以及具有竞争力的序列回收率（AAR 0.38）。

Conclusion: ConTact 在 CHIMERA-Bench 数据集上取得了最佳结构质量（RMSD 提升 7%）、最佳表位感知能力（F1 分数较 GNN 基线提升 10%），同时在序列回收率上具有竞争力（AAR 0.38）。该研究证明了将接触预测与氨基酸选择解耦的有效性，为抗体 CDR 设计提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Computational antibody CDR design methods condition on antigen structure to generate binding loops, yet existing architectures conflate two fundamentally distinct sub-problems: identifying which CDR positions will contact the antigen, and selecting amino acids at those positions. This conflation forces models to learn contact reasoning implicitly through uniform message passing, diluting antigen signal across all positions equally. We introduce ConTact, a contact-then-act architecture that explicitly decomposes CDR design into three cascaded stages: learning surface complementarity fingerprints, predicting CDR-antigen contacts, and injecting contact-gated antigen features into the sequence head. A distance-biased cross-attention module encodes geometric priors favoring spatial neighbors, while a contact-weighted cross-entropy loss concentrates gradient signal on binding-critical positions. On CHIMERA-Bench dataset, ConTact achieves the best structural quality (7% RMSD improvement over the next-best baseline), best epitope awareness (10% F1 score over GNN baselines), and competitive sequence recovery (AAR 0.38) among several CDR-H3 design baselines.

</details>


### [186] [When Are Teacher Tokens Reliable? Position-Weighted On-Policy Self-Distillation for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21606)
*Xiaogeng Liu,Xinyan Wang,Yingzi Ma,Yechao Zhang,Chaowei Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示了推理蒸馏中教师标记可靠性具有轨迹级结构，发现序列内位置评分（AUROC=0.83）优于局部不确定性评分（≤0.57），并基于此提出PW-OPSD方法，在多种模型上验证了有效性，无需额外教师计算即可提升数学推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准OPSD方法对所有生成token一视同仁，隐含假设教师目标在每个前缀处同等可靠，但推理场景中教师熵高可能反映不可行不确定性或良性解多样性，需要诊断工具区分这两种情况。

Method: 提出基于轨迹级结构的位置加权自蒸馏方法（PW-OPSD），使用within-sequence位置评分预测教师标记可靠性，在保持原有学生rollout、privileged teacher pass和裁剪前向KL目标的基础上，对序列中较后位置赋予更高的权重。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B上，序列内位置评分是教师标记可靠性的最强预测因子，AUROC达0.83；局部不确定性评分最高仅0.57。诊断衍生的PW-OPSD在AIME 2024和2025 Avg@12上分别提升+1.0和+1.1分；在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B和Olmo-3-7B-Think上也显示一致的性能改进。

Conclusion: PW-OPSD通过利用序列内位置评分来识别教师标记可靠性，实现了无需额外教师计算即可提升推理蒸馏效果的目标。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) trains a student on its own rollouts using a privileged teacher, but its standard objective weights all generated tokens equally, implicitly treating the privileged teacher target as equally reliable at every student-visited prefix. Existing entropy-based OPD methods relax this uniformity by modulating token-level supervision with teacher entropy, but high teacher entropy in reasoning has an ambiguous reliability meaning: it can reflect either non-viable uncertainty or benign solution diversity. To identify this phenomenon, we introduce a branch-viability diagnostic. Specifically, we record next-token alternatives from the privileged-answer teacher prompt, force each alternative after the student prompt plus its on-policy spine prefix, and test whether the resulting student-template continuation recovers the correct answer. On Qwen3-4B, we find that an oriented within-sequence position score is the strongest tested predictor of teacher-token reliability, reaching an area-under-ROC-curve (AUROC) of 0.83; local uncertainty scores are at most 0.57. Motivated by this trajectory-level structure, we propose Position-Weighted On-Policy Self-Distillation (PW-OPSD), which applies an increasing position weight while keeping the same student rollout, privileged teacher pass, and clipped forward-KL target as OPSD. In our comprehensive evaluations with different random seeds, the diagnostic-derived PW-OPSD improves AIME 2024 and AIME 2025 Avg@12 by +1.0 and +1.1 points, and a generalization evaluation on two larger-scale models from different families, DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B and Olmo-3-7B-Think, also demonstrates consistent aggregate Avg@12 improvements. These results show that teacher-token reliability in reasoning distillation is trajectory-structured and can be utilized without additional teacher computation.

</details>


### [187] [AgForce Enables Antigen-conditioned Generative Antibody Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21610)
*Mansoor Ahmed,Murray Patterson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AgForce是一种新型抗体设计架构，通过GNN编码器配合MDN+aMCL解码器和抗原循环一致性机制，解决了现有方法忽略抗原输入导致的三类失败模式，显著提升了抗原特异性CDR设计和序列恢复性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抗体设计方法虽然以抗原结构为条件，但在生成CDR时却大量忽略抗原输入。研究识别出三个导致此问题的失败模式：1）抗原盲区：模型从抗体框架上下文而非抗原信息推导预测；2）词汇塌陷：预测的氨基酸缩减至每位置仅3-5种，远低于天然分布；3）标准逐位置交叉熵训练必然收敛至位置边缘分布，无法产生抗原特异性序列预测。

Method: AgForce采用GNN作为编码器，配合用于序列-结构协同设计的专门解码器。关键技术创新包括：防止抗体捷径路径的框架dropout、门控瓶颈和双曲交叉注意力机制；用MDN序列头（具有类似Potts的成对耦合）和退火多选择学习（aMCL）替代交叉熵目标函数；以及通过抗原循环一致性头强制预测分布编码抗原身份。

Result: 在CHIMERA-Bench数据集上，AgForce同时实现了最佳结合质量和序列恢复率。氨基酸回收率比最强序列基线提高8%，在所有界面指标上均超越基线，并将GNN方法的有效词汇量几乎翻倍。

Conclusion: AgForce通过创新的编码器-解码器架构和替代性训练目标，成功解决了抗体设计中的抗原盲区和词汇塌陷问题，在CHIMERA-Bench基准上同时实现了最佳结合质量和序列恢复率，氨基酸回收率提升8%，有效词汇量几乎翻倍。

Abstract: Antibody design methods condition on antigen structure to generate complementarity-determining regions (CDR), yet a systematic evaluation of baseline methods reveals that they largely ignore the antigen input. We identify three failure modes that explain this behavior. Antigen blindness arises because models derive predictions from antibody framework context rather than antigen information, producing nearly identical CDRs regardless of the target. Vocabulary collapse reduces predicted amino acids to three to five per position, far below the ground truth distribution in native sequences. Moreover, any model trained with standard per-position cross-entropy converges to the positional marginal distribution, making it provably unable to produce antigen-specific sequence predictions. We propose a novel encoder-decoder architecture called AgForce, that uses a graph neural network (GNN) as the encoder and specialized decoders for sequence-structure co-design. Specifically, we apply framework dropout, gated bottlenecks, and hyperbolic cross attention that prevent the antibody shortcut path. In the decoder, a Mixture Density Network (MDN) sequence head with Potts-like pairwise coupling and annealed Multiple Choice Learning (aMCL) replaces the cross-entropy objective with a multi-component distribution whose optimal solution differs from the positional marginal. An antigen cycle consistency head routes gradients through the sequence decoder, forcing predicted distributions to encode antigen identity. AgForce achieves the best binding quality and sequence recovery simultaneously on the CHIMERA-Bench dataset, improving amino acid recovery by 8% over the strongest sequence baseline while surpassing the baselines across all interface metrics, and nearly doubling the effective vocabulary of GNN methods. The source code is available at: https://github.com/mansoor181/ag-force.git

</details>


### [188] [$\textit{BlockFormer}$ : Transformer-based inference from interaction maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21617)
*Eloïse Touron,Pedro L. C. Rodrigues,Julyan Arbel,Nelle Varoquaux,Michael Arbel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Inference from interaction maps, such as centromere identification from genome-wide chromosome conformation capture techniques -- notably Hi-C -- can be formulated as a generic inverse problem: infer a set of parameters given a map summarizing pairwise interactions between entities through blocks of variable numbers and sizes. In this work, we introduce a data-driven approach that leverages shared structure between these maps, such as global alignment between localized patterns, while handling the variability in number and size of entities arising in real-world data. Our approach relies on a transformer architecture capable of handling such variability and a custom simulator to generate abundant, yet computationally cheap synthetic data for training. Applied to the problem of centromere localization, the method accurately recovers their genomic positions across a wide range of species of various genome sizes.

</details>


### [189] [Alike Parts: A Feature-Informed Approach to Local and Global Prototype Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21646)
*Jacek Karolczak,Jerzy Stefanowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper proposes a two-level feature importance integration framework for prototype-based interpretability: "alike parts" for local explanations highlighting shared features, and augmented prototype selection promoting feature attribution diversity while maintaining fidelity.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Prototype-based explanations lack feature-level granularity, limiting their interpretability for understanding ML black box classifiers.

Method: Integrate feature importance at two levels: (1) "alike parts" method for local explanations using feature importance to identify shared features between an instance and its nearest prototype; (2) augment global prototype selection with feature importance term to promote diversity in feature attributions.

Result: Experiments on six benchmark datasets show the augmented selection maintains or increases prediction fidelity without compromising model performance.

Conclusion: The augmented prototype selection maintains or improves prediction fidelity while increasing feature diversity, demonstrating that feature-level granularity can be enhanced without compromising model performance.

Abstract: Prototype-based explanations offer an intuitive, example-based approach to support the interpretability of machine learning black box classifiers but often lack feature-level granularity. We introduce a framework that integrates feature importance at two levels to address this gap. First, for local explanations, we propose \textit{alike parts}: a method that uses feature importance scores to highlight the most relevant, shared feature subsets between a classified instance and its nearest prototype, guiding user attention. Second, we augment the global prototype selection objective function with a feature importance term to actively promote diversity in the feature attributions of the selected prototypes. Experiments on six benchmark datasets show that this augmented selection process maintains or, in some cases, increases the prediction fidelity of the surrogate model, suggesting that feature diversity does not compromise model fidelity.

</details>


### [190] [Dropout Universality: Scaling Laws and Optimal Scheduling at the Edge-of-Chaos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21648)
*Lucas Fernandez Sarmiento*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: A mean-field theory of dropout as a perturbation to critical signal propagation reveals that smooth vs. kinked activations form distinct universality classes with different critical exponents and universal scaling collapse. The framework yields optimal front-loaded dropout schedules via a rank-flow tie-breaker, improving test performance across MLPs and Vision Transformers.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dropout shifts the perfect-alignment fixed point, creating a gap in understanding how information propagates through deep networks at critical initialization. Existing theories lack a unified treatment of how dropout strength and network criticality interact across different activation functions.

Method: Mean-field theory treating dropout as a perturbation of critical signal propagation at the edge of chaos. Analytical derivation of critical and crossover scaling laws, identification of universality classes via correlation map analysis (Taylor expansion for smooth activations vs. branch point analysis for kinked activations), and universal two-parameter scaling collapse. Practical application: saturated dropout profile derivation and rank-flow tie-breaker for schedule selection.

Result: Critical and crossover scaling laws for correlation decay; smooth and kinked (ReLU-like) activations constitute distinct universality classes with different critical exponents; universal two-parameter scaling collapse in detuning and dropout strength; front-loaded dropout schedules selected via rank-flow tie-breaker achieve substantially reduced test loss with accuracy gains as secondary effect.

Conclusion: The framework unifies dropout theory across architectures, establishes a clear distinction between activation function universality classes, and provides practical scheduling strategies that improve test performance without additional computational cost. Predictions are validated across MLPs and Vision Transformers, with extensions to CNN/ResNet architectures discussed.

Abstract: We develop a mean-field theory of dropout as a perturbation of critical signal propagation at the edge of chaos. Dropout shifts the perfect-alignment fixed point, making the depth scale for information propagation finite even at critical initialization. We derive critical and crossover scaling laws for correlation decay and establish that smooth activations and kinked, ReLU-like activations constitute distinct universality classes, with different critical exponents and a universal two-parameter scaling collapse in detuning and dropout strength. The distinction traces to the analytic structure of the correlation map: smooth activations admit a Taylor expansion near perfect alignment, while kinked activations develop a branch point with universal non-analyticity. As a corollary, the framework yields saturated dropout profiles under fixed budget; a rank-flow tie-breaker then selects front-loaded schedules, substantially reducing held-out test loss at no extra computational cost, with accuracy gains as a consistent secondary effect. We test the predictions in MLPs and Vision Transformers and discuss CNN/ResNet extensions.

</details>


### [191] [EntmaxKV: Support-Aware Decoding for Entmax Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21649)
*Gonçalo Duarte,Miguel Couceiro,Marcos V. Treviso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EntmaxKV通过利用α-entmax的精确零值特性，在KV页面加载前利用稀疏性，结合查询感知评分、支持感知选择和高斯感知选择器，实现高效长上下文解码，在100万上下文长度下实现最高5.43倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文解码受KV-cache内存流量限制严重。现有稀疏解码方法专为softmax注意力设计，密集尾部特性导致任何截断都会丢弃非零概率质量。α-entmax可产生精确零值，使稀疏解码从近似变为支持恢复。

Method: 提出EntmaxKV框架，融合查询感知页面评分、支持感知候选选择和稀疏entmax注意力；引入高斯感知entmax选择器，从轻量页面统计估计entmax阈值，自适应调整所选预算。

Result: 丢弃更少概率质量、保留更多支持token、在相同KV预算下实现更低输出误差。在长上下文和语言建模基准测试中表现优异。

Conclusion: EntmaxKV在100万上下文长度下实现3.36×(softmax)和5.43×(entmax)加速，匹配全缓存entmax性能，同时仅使用KV缓存的一小部分。

Abstract: Long-context decoding is increasingly limited by KV-cache memory traffic since each generated token attends over a cache whose size grows linearly with context length. Existing sparse decoding methods reduce this cost by selecting subsets of tokens or pages, but are designed for softmax attention, whose dense tails make any truncation discard nonzero probability mass. In contrast, $α$-entmax produces exact zeros, turning sparse decoding from dense-tail approximation into support recovery: if the selected candidates contain the entmax support, sparse decoding remains exact. While recent entmax kernels enable efficient training, they do not address the autoregressive decoding bottleneck, where dense inference still streams the full KV cache before sparsity is known. In this work, we introduce EntmaxKV, an entmax-native sparse decoding framework that exploits sparsity before KV pages are loaded. EntmaxKV combines query-aware page scoring, support-aware candidate selection, and sparse entmax attention. We analyze truncation error through the dropped probability mass $δ$, showing that output error is controlled by $δ$ and vanishes when the entmax support is recovered. We further introduce a Gaussian-aware entmax selector that estimates the entmax threshold from lightweight page statistics, adapting the selected budget to the score distribution. Empirically, EntmaxKV drops less probability mass, retains more support tokens, and achieves lower output error than softmax-based sparse decoding at matched KV budgets. On long-context and language modeling benchmarks, it closely matches full-cache entmax while using a small fraction of the KV cache, achieving up to $3.36\times$ (softmax) and $5.43\times$ (entmax) speedup over full attention baselines at 1M context length. Code available at: https://github.com/deep-spin/entmaxkv.

</details>


### [192] [Amplifying, Not Learning: Fine-Tuned AI Text Detectors Amplify a Pretrained Direction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21653)
*Alexander Smirnov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AI文本检测器的本质机制是通过预训练的典型性轴进行判别，而非构建新的AI-vs-human边界；原始编码器投影即可达到或超过微调后的性能；该轴在非母语写作上反转，表明这是模型内在的典型性感知而非针对AI的特异性检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解AI文本检测器的实际工作机制——它们是否真的构建了AI-vs-human边界，还是只是放大了预训练的典型性轴。

Method: 在原始编码器上投射到centroid(AI)-centroid(HC3)轴；在三种架构（RoBERTa-base等）上验证；使用24-example冻结探测器；闭式Jacobian预测器参数化轴干预；三种操作不同的探测方法交叉验证。

Result: 在原始编码器上，该典型性轴已能达到AUROC 0.806-0.944；全量微调反而降低而非提升判别能力；同一轴在非母语ESL写作上反转（AUROC 0.06-0.20）；24-example冻结探测器与全量微调性能相当（0.900 vs 0.895）；闭式Jacobian预测器R²=1.000；ELECTRA-CE在FPR=1%下TPR从0.000提升至0.904。

Conclusion: LoRA到全量微调的偏差中≥97%是校准偏移而非习得表征；三种架构的探测方法（caps_rate残差化、几何符号ε消融、闭式文本对预测器）确认了observer不变性，cos相似度达0.74/0.81/1.00；在TPR=0.90匹配评估下，已发布的干预方法（CC、dealign-f2c）在27个单元格中等效（|Δ AUROC| ≤ 0.0081）。

Abstract: AI text detectors amplify a pretrained typicality axis; they do not construct an AI-vs-human boundary. On raw encoders before any task supervision, projecting onto centroid(AI)-centroid(HC3) achieves NYT-vs-HC3 AUROC 0.806/0.944/0.834 across three architectures (86-106% of the fine-tuned discrimination ceiling: on RoBERTa-base, raw projection exceeds fine-tuning); on RoBERTa-base, full fine-tuning reduces discrimination below raw on both fluent-formal populations tested. The same axis inverts on non-native ESL writing (AUROC 0.06-0.20) -- a falsifiable prediction unique to the typicality reading. A 24-example frozen probe matches full fine-tuning (0.900 vs 0.895). A closed-form Jacobian predictor parameterises axis-manipulating interventions with R^2 = 1.000 universal, lifts ELECTRA-CE deployment TPR from 0.000 to 0.904 at FPR = 1%, and transfers to three independently-trained third-party RoBERTa detectors at 16/16 oracle-equivalence (57% NYT-FPR reduction on the OpenAI detector). Scope: encoder family; mechanism magnitude HC3-anchored; population-level shared axis with per-text mechanisms varying across architectures. Three operationally distinct probes -- text-surface caps_rate residualisation, geometric signed-epsilon ablation, closed-form text-pair predictor -- agree at cos 0.74/0.81/1.00 across three architectures, confirming observer-invariance. Under matched-TPR-0.90 evaluation, the published intervention zoo (CC, dealign-f2c) is calibration-equivalent across 27 cells (|Delta AUROC| <= 0.0081), and >= 97% of the LoRA->full-FT bias gap on ELECTRA is calibration shift, not learned representation -- the central claim's prediction confirmed.

</details>


### [193] [Value-Gradient Hypothesis of RL for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21654)
*Arip Asadulaev,Daniil Ognev,Karim Salta,Martin Takac*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 从价值梯度视角为无评论家RL方法提供理论解释，揭示了注意力机制在估计价值信号中的作用，并给出了RL效益最大化的判断准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPO和GRPO等无评论家方法在LLM后训练中广泛应用，但其有效性机制尚不清晰，缺乏关于何时能获得最大收益的理论理解。

Method: 在可微分rollout和加性噪声参数化假设下，证明actor更新在期望上等价于价值梯度形式；利用注意力机制的自微分性质，通过注意力输出经验代价状态来近似价值信号。

Result: Actor更新可分解为价值梯度信号与可达奖励空间两部分；近似误差由采样间隔和策略熵控制。

Conclusion: 研究提出了价值梯度视角下无评论家RL何时最有效的判断准则，为沿着预训练轨迹选择RL应用时机提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning substantially improves pretrained language models, but it remains understudied why critic-free methods such as PPO and GRPO work as well as they do, and when they should provide the largest gains. We develop a value-gradient perspective of critic-free RL for LLM post-training. First, under a differentiable rollout and additive-noise parameterization, we show that the actor update is value-gradient-like in expectation: the backward pass propagates costates whose conditional expectation equals the value gradient. Second, for discrete transformer policies, we show that autodifferentiation through attention produces empirical costates that approximate this value signal, with an error controlled by the sampling gap and policy entropy. These results motivate a decomposition of RL impact into value gradient signal and reachable reward headroom, yielding a criterion for when RL should be most effective along a pretraining trajectory.

</details>


### [194] [Hierarchical Variational Policies for Reward-Guided Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21661)
*Kushagra Pandey,Farrin Marouf Sofian,Jan Niklas Groeneveld,Felix Draxler,Stephan Mandt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Proposes an amortized test-time adaptation framework for diffusion models using hierarchical variational inference, enabling fast few-step sampling while maintaining quality. Achieves >5x speedup on 4x super-resolution with better results, and SOTA quality via semi-amortized extension.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing methods for adapting pretrained diffusion models to downstream tasks (e.g., inverse problems) require expensive test-time guidance or optimization, making inference costly.

Method: Hierarchical variational framework that amortizes control into a lightweight stochastic policy; supports few-step diffusion sampling with large step sizes while maintaining quality through structured per-step control.

Result: On 4x super-resolution: better perceptual quality with over 5x faster inference than best baseline. State-of-the-art perceptual quality across multiple challenging inverse problems.

Conclusion: A fully amortized sampler achieving strong quality-speed tradeoff, with a semi-amortized extension that achieves SOTA perceptual quality across several inverse problems.

Abstract: Adapting pretrained diffusion models to downstream objectives such as inverse problems often requires expensive test-time guidance or optimization. We propose a principled framework for generating high-quality reward-aligned samples at substantially reduced inference cost. Our approach formulates test-time adaptation as a hierarchical variational model, where control is amortized into a lightweight yet expressive stochastic policy. This formulation naturally supports few-step diffusion sampling: large step sizes enable fast inference, while the learned policy maintains sample quality by providing structured per-step control. The resulting fully amortized sampler achieves a strong quality--speed tradeoff, matching or exceeding recent test-time scaling baselines while requiring significantly less compute. For example, on 4x super-resolution, our method achieves better perceptual quality with more than 5x faster inference compared to the best-performing baseline. We further extend our approach to a semi-amortized regime that combines cheap amortized proposals with limited test-time optimization, achieving state-of-the-art perceptual quality across several challenging inverse problems.

</details>


### [195] [TBP-mHC: full expressivity for manifold-constrained hyper connections through transportation polytopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21724)
*Anton Lyubinin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 作者提出Transportation Birkhoff Polytope (TBP)及其递归变体RTBP，将双随机混合矩阵的参数复杂度从阶乘降低到O((n-1)²)，同时保持 Birkhoff 多面体的完全表达能力，无需迭代归一化，在语言模型预训练中展现出竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing Hyper-Connection (HC) methods suffer from training instability due to unconstrained mixing. While mHC-lite ensures exact constraints via convex combinations of permutation matrices, it incurs factorial complexity. KromHC reduces cost but restricts mixing matrices to a structured submanifold, limiting expressivity.

Method: The authors propose Transportation Birkhoff Polytope (TBP) parameterizations and their Recursive variants (RTBP), which construct exactly doubly stochastic mixing matrices using (n-1)^2 degrees of freedom through a novel parameterization approach that avoids iterative normalization.

Result: Empirical results on language model pre-training demonstrate competitive performance with improved stability and scalability compared to prior approaches.

Conclusion: TBP and RTBP provide a scalable and stable alternative for hyper-connection mixing, achieving full expressivity of the Birkhoff polytope without iterative normalization or combinatorial explosion.

Abstract: Hyper-Connections (HC) improve residual networks by introducing learnable mixing across multiple residual streams, but unconstrained mixing leads to training instability. Manifold-Constrained Hyper-Connections (mHC) address this by enforcing approximate double stochasticity via Sinkhorn normalization, while mHC-lite ensures exact constraints through convex combinations of permutation matrices at the cost of factorial complexity. KromHC reduces this cost using Kronecker-product parameterizations, but restricts the mixing matrices to a structured submanifold of the Birkhoff polytope .
  We propose Transportation Birkhoff Polytope (TBP) parameterizations and their Recursive variants (RTBP), which construct exactly doubly stochastic mixing matrices with $(n-1)^2$ degrees of freedom. Our approach avoids iterative normalization and combinatorial explosion while preserving full expressivity of the Birkhoff polytope. Empirical results on language model pre-training' demonstrate competitive performance with improved stability and scalability.

</details>


### [196] [I-SAFE: Wasserstein Coherence Metrics for Structural Auditing of Scientific AI Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21731)
*Barbara Tarantino,Gennaro Auricchio,Paolo Giudici*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出I-SAFE框架，通过Wasserstein Coherence Metric评估科学AI模型在结构引导扰动下的输出分布一致性，以检测模型是否真正学习到领域相关结构而非利用快捷特征。在DTI预测任务上的实验表明，基于准确率的评估会掩盖模型间本质差异，而I-SAFE能够揭示这些差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度学习模型在科学预测任务中表现出高基准性能，但这种高性能可能源于模型利用了快捷特征、数据集特定规律或分布偏差，而非真正捕捉到领域相关的结构。这导致强基准性能被脆弱地解读为科学有意义行为的证据。

Method: I-SAFE框架通过Wasserstein Coherence Metric (WCM)为核心，采用后验分布审计策略。在给定黑盒预测器和外部结构先验的条件下，对输入进行结构引导扰动，评估模型输出的分布一致性。框架使用三个互补指标：QBM（分位数度量）评估位置级一致性，WCM评估序数一致性，翻译不变WCM变体评估形状一致性。

Result: 在药物-靶标相互作用(DTI)预测任务上，使用Davis激酶基准、KLIFS结合位点注释和三种序列模型（DeepConvDTI、DeepDTA、TAPB）进行验证。结果显示，尽管模型在传统精度指标上表现相当，但I-SAFE揭示了显著不同的分布响应特征，这些差异在传统基于准确率的评估中完全不可见。

Conclusion: I-SAFE框架是模型无关的，适用于任何具有结构化输入分解和外部先验的领域，为科学AI模型的可靠性评估提供了新范式。

Abstract: Deep learning models are increasingly used in scientific prediction tasks where strong benchmark performance is often interpreted as evidence of scientifically meaningful behavior. This interpretation is fragile, as models may exploit shortcut features, dataset-specific regularities, or distributional biases that are predictive on held-out data but not aligned with domain-relevant structure. To address this limitation, we introduce the \textsc{I-SAFE} (Interventional Secure, Accurate, Fair and Explainable) framework, a post-hoc distributional auditing framework for scientific AI models centered on the Wasserstein Coherence Metric (WCM). Given a trained black-box predictor and an external structural prior encoding domain knowledge about task-relevant input structure, \textsc{I-SAFE} evaluates raw model outputs under structurally guided perturbations of the input. The proposed audit measures output-distribution coherence through three complementary metrics: a Quantile-Based Metric (QBM) for location-level coherence, the WCM for ordinal coherence, and a translation-invariant WCM variant for shape coherence. We instantiate \textsc{I-SAFE} on drug--target interaction (DTI) prediction using the Davis kinase benchmark, KLIFS (Kinase--Ligand Interaction Fingerprints and Structures) binding-pocket annotations, and three sequence-based DTI models: DeepConvDTI, DeepDTA, and TAPB. Although the models operate in a comparable predictive regime, \textsc{I-SAFE} reveals substantially different distributional response profiles, a distinction invisible to accuracy-based evaluation. The framework is model-agnostic and applicable to any domain where inputs admit a structured decomposition and an external prior is available.

</details>


### [197] [Correcting Class Imbalance in Prior-Data Fitted Networks for Tabular Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21742)
*Samuel McDowell,Nathan Stromberg,Lalitha Sankar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究针对PFN在表格分类中的类别不平衡问题，探索了多种传统不平衡处理技术的适配方案，发现阈值调整和下采样两种方法能有效提升稀有类别的分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PFN在表格分类任务中表现优异，但面临类别不平衡问题导致稀有类别性能下降；传统技术因PFN的上下文学习机制无法使用基于损失函数的策略，且其他技术效果未经证实

Method: 将多种传统类别不平衡处理技术（阈值调整、下采样等）适配至PFN框架并进行分析

Result: 阈值调整和下采样两种技术均表现良好

Conclusion: 阈值调整表现优异（得益于PFN的校准特性），下采样表现相当（得益于PFN的有限数据性能优势），且下采样还具有降低推理计算成本的额外好处

Abstract: Prior-data fitted networks (PFNs) have achieved exceptional performance on tabular classification tasks. However, like other classifiers, their performance can suffer under the effect of class imbalance, resulting in poor performance for rare classes. Several techniques exist which attempt to mitigate the deleterious effect of class imbalance on classification performance, but the in-context learning (ICL) dynamic of PFNs means that loss-based strategies are impossible, and other techniques are unproven. We have adapted several classical techniques addressing class imbalance and analyzed their performance on PFN classification. We observe that thresholding performs exceptionally well because of the calibration characteristics of PFNs, and downsampling performs comparably because of PFNs exceptional limited-data performance, with the additional benefit of reduced computation cost for inference.

</details>


### [198] [Quantitative coronary calcification analysis for prediction of myocardial ischemia using non-contrast CT calcium scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21745)
*Juhwan Lee,Sadeer Al-Kindi,Ammar Hoori,Tao Hu,Hao Wu,Justin N. Kim,Robert Gilkeson,Sanjay Rajagopalan,David L. Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一种基于机器学习的框架，利用常规非对比CT扫描预测心肌缺血。结果表明钙omics特征(而非仅Agatston评分)可显著提升预测性能，为更便捷的心血管风险评估提供了新工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统非对比CT钙化评分(CTCS)用于心血管风险分层已有明确价值，但常规Agatston评分对心肌缺血的预测能力有限，需要引入更丰富的钙相关特征以提升预测精度。

Method: 回顾性分析1375例患者数据，使用XGBoost+SHAP进行特征选择，5折交叉验证训练预测模型，纳入74个变量(含临床、Agatston评分、钙omics)，结合逻辑回归进行关联分析。

Result: 987例患者中9%存在心肌缺血。模型(Agatston+8个钙omics特征+年龄)精确率98.9±3.0%，敏感性79.2±8.4%，F1 87.7±5.3%。钙omics特征显著优于仅用临床变量或临床+Agatston的模型(p<0.05)。钙化动脉数量虽SHAP排名最低，但logistic回归显示与心肌缺血最强相关(OR 3.63, 95%CI 2.80-4.77)。

Conclusion: 钙omics特征在常规非对比CTCS扫描中具有增量预测价值，可支持更便捷的心血管风险分层。

Abstract: Non-contrast computed tomography calcium scoring (CTCS) is widely recognized as an effective tool for cardiovascular risk stratification. This study aimed to develop a novel machine learning framework for predicting myocardial ischemia from routine non-contrast CTCS scans using quantitative coronary calcium assessment. This study analyzed 1,375 patients who underwent both non-contrast CTCS and regadenoson stress cardiac positron emission tomography myocardial perfusion imaging within one year at University Hospitals Cleveland Medical Center. A total of 74 variables, including clinical variables, Agatston score, and calcium-omics features, were evaluated. Relevant features were identified using XGBoost with Shapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP). Predictive models were trained and evaluated using 5-fold cross-validation. Among 987 patients, 89 (9%) were positive for myocardial ischemia. The final model incorporated the Agatston score, eight calcium-omics features, and age. The proposed model achieved a precision of 98.9+/-3.0%, sensitivity of 79.2+/-8.4, and F1 score of 87.7+/-5.3%. The addition of calcium-omics features significantly improved predictive performance compared with models using clinical variables alone or clinical variables with the Agatston score (p<0.05). Interestingly, the number of calcified arteries, despite being the lowest-ranked feature based on SHAP analysis, showed the strongest association with myocardial ischemia in logistic regression analysis (odds ratio: 3.63, 95% confidence interval: 2.80-4.77, p<0.00001). We developed a machine learning approach for predicting myocardial ischemia using routinely acquired non-contrast CTCS scans. Calcium-omics features provided incremental predictive value beyond conventional risk factors and Agatston scoring and may support more accessible cardiovascular risk stratification.

</details>


### [199] [Models Can Model, But Can't Bind: Structured Grounding in Text-to-Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21751)
*Zhiqi Gao,Albert Ge,Alexander Berenbeim,Nathaniel D. Bastian,Frederic Sala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Text2Opt需要建模和绑定两种可分离能力。通过Text2Opt-Bench基准测试发现数据规模增大导致精度急剧下降的"有效绑定限制"现象。提出BIND方法将数值数据外部化，显著提升性能；验证了binding作为独立任务的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本转优化任务面临建模和绑定两大挑战；随着实例数据规模增大，模型精度急剧下降（有效绑定限制问题），即使 formulation 本身简单。

Method: 提出BIND方法——将数值数据外部化到结构化文件，使模型以程序化方式绑定而非从提示中转录；探索仅在binding任务上微调的专门模型。

Result: BIND使GPT-5-Nano从59.1%提升至82.4%，GPT-5从86.2%提升至95.8%；绑定专家模型在三个不同优化类别上超越端到端SFT和RL方法。

Conclusion: BIND通过程序化绑定数值数据显著提升性能；1.5B绑定专家模型可匹配7B端到端基线，证明binding是可分离且值得独立优化的核心能力。

Abstract: Text-to-optimization requires two separable capabilities: modeling -- choosing the right optimization structure -- and binding -- grounding every coefficient, index, and parameter in the concrete problem data. We study this via Text2Opt-Bench, a scalable benchmark of solver-verified optimization problems spanning 12 categories, from textbook linear programs to stochastic and multi-objective formulations with up to thousands of variables. Across 10+ models, we find that accuracy collapses as instance data grows, even when the formulation itself is simple. We call this the effective binding limit. We address this via a simple inference-time approach, BIND, which externalizes numeric data to structured files so the model binds data programmatically rather than transcribing from the prompt. BIND improves GPT-5-Nano from 59.1% to 82.4% accuracy, matching pass@5 (82.0%) at lower token cost than pass@1, and GPT-5 from 86.2% to 95.8%. Furthermore, we validate our hypothesis by finetuning a model exclusively on binding and show that it outperforms end-to-end SFT and RL across three structurally distinct optimization categories, with a 1.5B binding specialist alone matching a 7B end-to-end baseline.

</details>


### [200] [PEARL: Unbiased Percentile Estimation via Contrastive Learning for Industrial-Scale Livestream Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21752)
*Blake Gella,Wei Wu,Yuhao Yin,Zexi Huang,Zikai Wang,Emily Liu,Junlin Zhang,Wentao Guo,Qinglei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PEARL addresses behavioral intensity imbalance in recommender systems by modeling relative preference signals through nonparametric contrastive percentile approximation, achieving significant improvements in both offline experiments and production deployment.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: User interaction data in recommender systems suffers from behavioral intensity imbalance—a systematic distortion where heterogeneous engagement patterns across users cause observed interactions to no longer faithfully reflect true preferences. This leads to models disproportionately amplifying signals from highly active users while underrepresenting others, ultimately degrading recommendation quality and robustness at scale.

Method: PEARL employs relative advantage debiasing with real contrastive interaction samples to directly approximate percentile relationships, without auxiliary distribution estimation. Key innovations include: (1) modeling relative preference signals instead of absolute engagement magnitudes, (2) a prediction-based bootstrapping mechanism for percentile smoothing to handle sparse and discrete feedback, (3) a generalized value-weighted formulation, and (4) a co-training strategy for enhanced representation learning.

Result: Extensive offline experiments demonstrate that PEARL effectively mitigates behavioral bias and consistently improves recommendation performance across multiple ranking targets. Online A/B testing in production (billions of users) confirms real-world gains: +2.10% Watch Duration, +0.80% Consumption Amount, +1.49% Interaction Rate, and -6.91% Report Rate.

Conclusion: PEARL achieves significant improvements in offline experiments and real-world deployment, with A/B testing showing +2.10% Watch Duration, +0.80% Consumption Amount, +1.49% Interaction Rate, and -6.91% Report Rate in a production livestream platform serving billions of users.

Abstract: Recommender systems trained on user interaction data are susceptible to behavioral intensity imbalance--a systematic distortion arising from heterogeneous engagement patterns across users. This imbalance skews feedback signals such that observed interactions no longer faithfully reflect true preferences, causing models to disproportionately amplify signals from highly active users while underrepresenting others, which ultimately degrades recommendation quality and robustness at scale. To address this issue, we propose a nonparametric contrastive percentile approximation framework, PEARL, that models relative preference signals instead of absolute engagement magnitudes. Building upon relative advantage debiasing, PEARL leverages real contrastive interaction samples to approximate percentile relationships directly, without relying on auxiliary distribution estimation models. We provide theoretical justification demonstrating that such pairwise comparisons yield unbiased estimates of percentile-based preference signals. For broader applicability, we introduce a prediction-based bootstrapping mechanism for percentile smoothing to handle sparse and discrete feedback, alongside a generalized value-weighted formulation and a co-training strategy to enhance both modeling flexibility and representation learning. Extensive offline experiments demonstrate that PEARL effectively mitigates behavioral bias and consistently improves recommendation performance across multiple ranking targets. Deployed in a production livestream platform with a combined user base of billions, online A/B testing confirms substantial real-world gains: +2.10% Watch Duration, +0.80% Consumption Amount, +1.49% Interaction Rate, and -6.91% Report Rate.

</details>


### [201] [Machine learning prediction of obstructive coronary artery disease using opportunistic coronary calcium and epicardial fat assessments from CT calcium scoring scans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21762)
*Juhwan Lee,Ammar Hoori,Tao Hu,Justin N. Kim,Mohamed H. E. Makhlouf,Michelle C. Williams,David E. Newby,Robert Gilkeson,Sanjay Rajagopalan,David L. Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一种基于CTCS图像的机器学习框架，通过整合钙-omics和脂肪-omics特征预测阻塞性CAD。在1,324例患者中，该模型以14个精选特征实现了85.3%的准确率，可靠预测了包括零钙积分但存在阻塞性CAD的患者，有望减少对侵入性诊断的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非对比CT钙化评分(CTCS)是检测冠状动脉钙化的成本效益较高的成像方式，本研究旨在利用CTCS图像中的冠状动脉钙化和心外膜脂肪的定量分析，开发机器学习框架以预测阻塞性冠状动脉疾病(CAD)。

Method: 研究纳入SCOT-HEART临床试验中1,324名同时接受CTCS和冠脉CT血管造影的患者。提取424个候选特征（24个临床变量、189个钙-omics特征、211个脂肪-omics特征）。采用CatBoost算法结合SHAP值进行特征选择，利用CatBoost梯度提升方法建立预测模型。

Result: 通过CatBoost-SHAP方法从424个候选特征中筛选出14个最具预测性的特征，其中2个来自脂肪-omics，12个来自钙-omics。优化后的模型达到灵敏度83.1±4.6%、特异度93.8±1.7%、准确率85.3±2.0%、F1分数73.9±3.3%的稳健预测能力。

Conclusion: 该框架在低至中等风险患者群体中具有显著的临床应用潜力，可减少对对比增强或侵入性诊断程序的依赖。脂肪-omics和钙-omics数据的整合显著提升了预测性能，且模型对零钙积分但患有阻塞性CAD的患者仍具有可靠的预测准确性。

Abstract: Non-contrast computed tomography calcium scoring (CTCS) is a cost-effective imaging modality widely used to detect coronary artery calcifications. This study aimed to develop an advanced machine learning framework that utilizes quantitative analyses of coronary calcium and epicardial fat from CTCS images to predict obstructive coronary artery disease (CAD). The study population consisted of 1,324 patients from the SCOT-HEART clinical trial who underwent both CTCS and coronary CT angiography. We extracted and analyzed a broad range of features, including 24 clinical variables, 189 calcium-omics, and 211 epicardial fat-omics features from the CTCS images. Feature selection was conducted using the CatBoost algorithm combined with SHapley Additive exPlanation (SHAP) values. Predictive modeling utilized the CatBoost gradient boosting method, focusing on the most informative features. From an initial set of 424 candidate features, 14 were identified as most predictive through the CatBoost-SHAP method. The top two predictive features originated from fat-omics, with the remaining 12 features derived from calcium-omics. The optimized model achieved robust predictive capabilities, demonstrating a sensitivity of 83.1+/-4.6%, specificity of 93.8+/-1.7%, accuracy of 85.3+/-2.0%, and an F1 score of 73.9+/-3.3%. Inclusion of calcium-omics and fat-omics data significantly improved predictive performance. Notably, the model also showed reliable predictive accuracy in patients with diverse coronary calcium scores, including cases with obstructive CAD despite a zero-calcium score. This innovative approach holds promise for improving clinical decision-making and potentially reducing dependence on contrast-enhanced or invasive diagnostic procedures, particularly within low-to intermediate-risk patient groups.

</details>


### [202] [On the Sample Complexity of Discounted Reinforcement Learning with Optimized Certainty Equivalents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21763)
*Oliver Mortensen,Mohammad Sadegh Talebi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限折扣MDP中基于OCE族风险测度的风险敏感强化学习样本复杂度问题。证明了utility函数定义域是否为ℝ是PAC可学习性的关键判据，建立了值函数学习和策略学习的PAC样本复杂度上界及关于SA的紧密下界，特别对CVaR_τ澄清了τ的依赖关系为1/τ²。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在有限折扣MDP中，基于优化确定性等价(OCE)族风险测度的样本复杂度问题。OCE族包含重要风险测度如熵风险、CVaR和均值-方差，但哪些OCE目标函数可以高效学习尚不明确。

Method: 采用模型基础的分析方法，推导PAC样本复杂度界。通过构造反例证明非PAC可学习性的下界，并针对受限utility函数类建立包含有效水平度1/(1-γ)依赖的更精确下界。

Result: 精确刻画了使OCE可PAC学习的utility函数类；证明当dom(u)≠ℝ时问题不可PAC学习；对于CVaR_τ，证得τ的正确依赖为1/τ²，相比最新结果改进了一个1/τ因子。

Conclusion: 论文建立了风险敏感RL中PAC学习的完整理论框架，证明了utility函数域的特性(是否等于ℝ)是PAC可学习性的关键判据，并给出了关于状态-动作空间规模SA的紧密下界。对于CVaR_τ，指出了τ的依赖关系应为1/τ²，改进现有结果。

Abstract: We study risk-sensitive reinforcement learning in finite discounted MDPs, where a generative model of the MDP is assumed to be available. We consider a family or risk measures called the optimized certainty equivalent (OCE), which includes important risk measures such as entropic risk, CVaR, and mean-variance. Our focus is on the sample complexities of learning the optimal state-action value function (value learning) and an optimal policy (policy learning) under recursive OCE. We provide an exact characterization of utility functions $u$ for which the corresponding OCE defines an objective that is PAC-learnable. We analyze a simple model-based approach and derive PAC sample complexity bounds. We establish that whenever $u$ does not have full domain $\text{dom}(u)\neq \mathbb{R}$, the corresponding problem is not PAC-learnable. Finally, we establish corresponding lower bounds for both value and policy learning, demonstrating tightness in the size $SA$ of state-action space, and for a more restricted class of utilities, we derive lower bounds that makes the dependence on the effective horizon $\frac{1}{1-γ}$ explicit. Specifically, for $\text{CVaR}_τ$ we show that the correct dependence on $τ$ is $\frac{1}{τ^2}$, thus improving by a factor of $\frac{1}τ$ over state-of-the-art although our bound has a suboptimal dependence on $\frac{1}{1-γ}$.

</details>


### [203] [Position: The Time for Sampling Is Now! Charting a New Course for Bayesian Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21765)
*Emanuel Sommer,David Rügamer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: The practical adoption of sampling-based inference (SAI) in Bayesian neural networks (BNNs) remains limited, partly due to persistent misconceptions about the feasibility and efficiency of sampling. This position paper argues that SAI has achieved computational parity with optimization-based methods and is at the verge of superseding such methods for effective and efficient inference in BNNs. This development should be in the interest of the whole community, promoting BNNs as a principled paradigm with its long-standing yet unfulfilled promise of providing principled uncertainty quantification for neural networks. SAI can even do more -- yielding superior prediction performance through model averaging, serving as the foundation for a plethora of possible downstream tasks, and providing crucial insights into the landscape of BNNs. In order to make such a change happen and unfold the potential of sampling, overcoming current misconceptions is a necessary first step. The next step is to realign research efforts toward addressing remaining challenges in SAI. In particular, the community must focus on two core problems: sufficient exploration of the posterior landscape and high-fidelity distillation of posterior samples for efficient downstream inference. By addressing conceptual and practical obstacles, we can unlock the full potential of SAI and establish it as a central tool in Bayesian deep learning.

</details>


### [204] [Provable Robustness against Backdoor Attacks via the Primal-Dual Perspective on Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21780)
*Aman Saxena,Jan Schuchardt,Yan Scholten,Stephan Günnemann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将随机平滑与差分隐私通过隐私配置文件连接，实现异构机制的有效组合，从而在统一框架下同时认证训练时和推理时的后门攻击鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
